===================================================
FILE: src\boss\boss_lunareth.gd
===================================================

class_name BossLunareth
extends CharacterBody2D

## Boss Lunareth — Linh Lộc Nguyệt Thực (M4)
## Linh thú bảo vệ Rừng Noctis bị ma lực hắc nguyệt tha hóa, cần được thanh tẩy.
## Quản lý State Machine FSM, hai đòn đánh, chuyển giai đoạn Phase 2, và cơ chế hồi sinh/reset.

const BossVisualScript = preload("res://src/boss/boss_visual.gd")

signal boss_state_changed(new_state_name: String)
signal boss_phase_changed(new_phase: int)
signal boss_health_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal boss_telegraph_started(skill_name: String, duration: float)
signal boss_defeated()

enum State {
	DORMANT,           # Chưa kích hoạt
	INTRO,             # Thức tỉnh / Thị uy khi Player bước vào đấu trường
	APPROACH,          # Tiếp cận Player
	TELEGRAPH,         # Cảnh báo trước đòn đánh (ít nhất 0.6s - 0.8s)
	ATTACK,            # Thi triển đòn đánh (Húc hoặc Sóng xung kích)
	RECOVERY,          # Thời gian nghỉ / Cơ hội phản công cho Player
	PHASE_TRANSITION,  # Chuyển đổi sang Giai đoạn 2 (khi HP <= 160)
	DEFEATED           # Đã thanh tẩy thành công
}

enum AttackType {
	HORN_CHARGE,       # Đòn 1: Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích (20 DMG, telegraph 0.7s)
	MOON_SHOCKWAVE     # Đòn 2: Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba (25 DMG, telegraph 0.9s)
}

@export var max_hp: float = 320.0
@export var move_speed_p1: float = 160.0
@export var move_speed_p2: float = 195.0
@export var charge_speed_p1: float = 550.0
@export var charge_speed_p2: float = 620.0
@export var recovery_time_p1: float = 1.4
@export var recovery_time_p2: float = 1.1 # Giảm ~21% thời gian nghỉ ở Phase 2
@export var gravity: float = 1100.0

@onready var health_component: HealthComponent = $HealthComponent
@onready var hurtbox: Hurtbox = $Hurtbox
@onready var charge_hitbox: Hitbox = $ChargeHitbox
@onready var boss_visual: Node2D = $BossVisual
@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D

var current_state: State = State.DORMANT
var current_attack: AttackType = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE
var is_phase_2: bool = false
var facing_direction: int = -1
var target_player: Player = null

var _state_timer: float = 0.0
var _charge_timer: float = 0.0
var _phase_2_triggered: bool = false
var _is_defeated: bool = false
var _attack_alternate: bool = false
var _initial_spawn_position: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO

var shockwave_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/boss/boss_shockwave.tscn")

func _ready() -> void:
	_initial_spawn_position = global_position
	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
		charge_hitbox.damage = 20.0
		charge_hitbox.owner_node = self

	if health_component:
		health_component.max_health = max_hp
		health_component.current_health = max_hp
		health_component.health_changed.connect(_on_health_changed)
		health_component.died.connect(_on_died)

	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.hit_received.connect(_on_hit_received)

	_change_state(State.DORMANT)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_on_floor():
		velocity.y += gravity * delta

	# Kiểm tra người chơi
	if target_player == null or not is_instance_valid(target_player):
		_find_player()

	match current_state:
		State.DORMANT:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			move_and_slide()

		State.INTRO:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.APPROACH)
			move_and_slide()

		State.APPROACH:
			_process_approach(delta)

		State.TELEGRAPH:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.ATTACK)
			move_and_slide()

		State.ATTACK:
			_process_attack(delta)

		State.RECOVERY:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			# Boss không tấn công khi Player đã chết
			if _is_player_dead():
				_state_timer = max(_state_timer, 0.5)
			elif _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.APPROACH)
			move_and_slide()

		State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_change_state(State.RECOVERY)
			move_and_slide()

		State.DEFEATED:
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 900.0 * delta)
			move_and_slide()

func _find_player() -> void:
	var players = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("players")
	if players.size() > 0 and players[0] is Player:
		target_player = players[0] as Player

func _is_player_dead() -> bool:
	if target_player == null or not is_instance_valid(target_player):
		return true
	var p_health: HealthComponent = target_player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
	return p_health == null or not p_health.is_alive()

func _update_facing() -> void:
	if target_player and is_instance_valid(target_player):
		var dx: float = target_player.global_position.x - global_position.x
		if abs(dx) > 15.0:
			facing_direction = 1 if dx > 0 else -1
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_facing"):
				boss_visual.set_facing(facing_direction)
			if charge_hitbox:
				charge_hitbox.position.x = 40.0 * facing_direction

func _process_approach(delta: float) -> void:
	if _is_player_dead():
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 600.0 * delta)
		move_and_slide()
		return

	_update_facing()
	var spd: float = move_speed_p2 if is_phase_2 else move_speed_p1
	velocity.x = facing_direction * spd

	var dist: float = abs(target_player.global_position.x - global_position.x)
	_state_timer -= delta

	# Quyết định tấn công nếu đã tiếp cận đủ gần hoặc timer tiếp cận hết hạn (~2.0s)
	if dist <= 320.0 or _state_timer <= 0.0:
		_decide_next_attack(dist)
		_change_state(State.TELEGRAPH)

	move_and_slide()

func _decide_next_attack(dist: float) -> void:
	# Luân phiên đòn đánh để tạo nhịp điệu chiến đấu công bằng
	_attack_alternate = not _attack_alternate
	if dist > 380.0:
		# Khoảng cách xa: Ưu tiên Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích
		current_attack = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE
	elif dist < 180.0:
		# Khoảng cách gần: Ưu tiên Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba
		current_attack = AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE
	else:
		current_attack = AttackType.HORN_CHARGE if _attack_alternate else AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE

func _process_attack(delta: float) -> void:
	match current_attack:
		AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
			var charge_spd: float = charge_speed_p2 if is_phase_2 else charge_speed_p1
			velocity.x = facing_direction * charge_spd
			_charge_timer -= delta
			if _charge_timer <= 0.0:
				_finish_attack()
			move_and_slide()
		AttackType.MOON_SHOCKWAVE:
			# Đòn chấn ba đã spawn khi vào state ATTACK
			velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, 800.0 * delta)
			_state_timer -= delta
			if _state_timer <= 0.0:
				_finish_attack()
			move_and_slide()

func _finish_attack() -> void:
	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
	_change_state(State.RECOVERY)

func _change_state(new_state: State) -> void:
	current_state = new_state
	var state_name: String = _get_state_name(current_state)
	emit_signal("boss_state_changed", state_name)

	match current_state:
		State.DORMANT:
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.IDLE)
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", false)
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", false)

		State.INTRO:
			_state_timer = 1.2
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.IDLE)
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", true)
				hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", true)

		State.APPROACH:
			_state_timer = 2.2
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.MOVE)

		State.TELEGRAPH:
			_update_facing()
			if current_attack == AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
				_state_timer = 0.7 # Đạt chuẩn tối thiểu 0.6s
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE)
				emit_signal("boss_telegraph_started", "Nguyệt Giác Xung Kích", _state_timer)
			else:
				_state_timer = 0.9 # Đạt chuẩn tối thiểu 0.8s
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE)
				emit_signal("boss_telegraph_started", "Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba", _state_timer)

		State.ATTACK:
			if current_attack == AttackType.HORN_CHARGE:
				_charge_timer = 0.45
				if charge_hitbox:
					charge_hitbox.reset_hits()
					charge_hitbox.damage = 20.0
					charge_hitbox.monitoring = true
					charge_hitbox.visible = true
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.ATTACK_CHARGE)
			else:
				_state_timer = 0.35
				_spawn_shockwaves()
				if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
					boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.ATTACK_STOMP)

		State.RECOVERY:
			_state_timer = recovery_time_p2 if is_phase_2 else recovery_time_p1
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.RECOVERY)

		State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			_state_timer = 1.3
			is_phase_2 = true
			if health_component:
				health_component.is_invulnerable = true
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION)
			emit_signal("boss_phase_changed", 2)

		State.DEFEATED:
			_is_defeated = true
			velocity = Vector2.ZERO
			if charge_hitbox:
				charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
				charge_hitbox.visible = false
			if hurtbox:
				hurtbox.monitoring = false
				hurtbox.monitorable = false
				var h_col := hurtbox.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
				if h_col:
					h_col.disabled = true
			if health_component:
				health_component.is_invulnerable = true
			if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("set_state"):
				boss_visual.set_state(BossVisualScript.VisualState.DEFEATED)
			emit_signal("boss_defeated")

func _spawn_shockwaves() -> void:
	if shockwave_scene == null:
		return
	var wave_dir: float = float(facing_direction)
	var sw = shockwave_scene.instantiate()
	if sw is Node2D:
		sw.global_position = Vector2(global_position.x + wave_dir * 30.0, global_position.y)
		if sw.has_method("setup"):
			sw.setup(wave_dir, is_phase_2)
		get_parent().add_child(sw)

func _on_hit_received(damage_info: DamageInfo) -> void:
	if current_state == State.DORMANT or current_state == State.DEFEATED or current_state == State.PHASE_TRANSITION:
		return
	if boss_visual and boss_visual.has_method("flash_hit"):
		boss_visual.flash_hit()

func _on_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	emit_signal("boss_health_changed", cur, max_v)

	# Kích hoạt Phase 2 khi HP <= 50% (<= 160) - Chỉ kích hoạt duy nhất một lần
	if not _phase_2_triggered and cur <= (max_v * 0.5) and cur > 0.0:
		_phase_2_triggered = true
		_change_state(State.PHASE_TRANSITION)

func _on_died() -> void:
	if not _is_defeated:
		_change_state(State.DEFEATED)

func activate_boss() -> void:
	if current_state == State.DORMANT and not _is_defeated:
		_change_state(State.INTRO)

func reset_boss() -> void:
	global_position = _initial_spawn_position
	velocity = Vector2.ZERO
	_phase_2_triggered = false
	_is_defeated = false
	is_phase_2 = false

	if health_component:
		health_component.current_health = max_hp
		health_component.is_invulnerable = false
		emit_signal("boss_health_changed", max_hp, max_hp)

	if charge_hitbox:
		charge_hitbox.monitoring = false
		charge_hitbox.visible = false
		charge_hitbox.reset_hits()

	if hurtbox:
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitoring", true)
		hurtbox.set_deferred("monitorable", true)

	emit_signal("boss_phase_changed", 1)
	_change_state(State.DORMANT)

func purify_boss() -> void:
	_is_defeated = true
	_phase_2_triggered = true
	if health_component:
		health_component.current_health = 0.0
	_change_state(State.DEFEATED)

func _get_state_name(st: State) -> String:
	match st:
		State.DORMANT: return "DORMANT"
		State.INTRO: return "INTRO"
		State.APPROACH: return "APPROACH"
		State.TELEGRAPH: return "TELEGRAPH"
		State.ATTACK: return "ATTACK"
		State.RECOVERY: return "RECOVERY"
		State.PHASE_TRANSITION: return "PHASE_TRANSITION"
		State.DEFEATED: return "DEFEATED"
		_: return "UNKNOWN"

FILE: src\boss\boss_shockwave.gd

class_name BossShockwave
extends Area2D

## Sóng ma lực Hắc Nguyệt Chấn Ba của Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Lan truyền trên mặt đất, gây 25 sát thương, có thể nhảy qua để né.

@export var speed: float = 380.0
@export var damage: float = 25.0
@export var knockback_force: float = 240.0
@export var direction: float = -1.0
@export var max_lifetime: float = 2.8

var _time_alive: float = 0.0
var _hit_nodes: Array[Node] = []

@onready var visual_wave: ColorRect = $VisualWave
@onready var core_glow: ColorRect = $VisualWave/CoreGlow

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Player Hurtbox nằm trên Layer 2
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)

func setup(p_direction: float, p_is_phase_2: bool = false) -> void:
	direction = sign(p_direction)
	if direction == 0.0:
		direction = -1.0
	if p_is_phase_2:
		speed = 440.0
		if visual_wave:
			visual_wave.color = Color(0.95, 0.15, 0.85, 0.9)
		if core_glow:
			core_glow.color = Color(1.0, 0.7, 1.0, 1.0)
	else:
		speed = 380.0
		if visual_wave:
			visual_wave.color = Color(0.3, 0.75, 1.0, 0.9)
		if core_glow:
			core_glow.color = Color(0.8, 0.95, 1.0, 1.0)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	_time_alive += delta
	position.x += direction * speed * delta

	# Hiệu ứng gợn sóng nhấp nhô
	if visual_wave:
		visual_wave.scale.y = 1.0 + sin(_time_alive * 25.0) * 0.15

	if _time_alive >= max_lifetime:
		queue_free()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node in _hit_nodes:
			return # Đã trúng rồi, không gây sát thương trùng

		var knock_dir := Vector2(direction, -0.3).normalized()
		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage, self, knock_dir, knockback_force)
		var success := hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		if success:
			_hit_nodes.append(hurtbox.owner_node)
			# Tạo hiệu ứng va chạm nhẹ và biến mất sau khi đâm trúng
			queue_free()

===================================================
FILE: src\boss\boss_visual.gd
===================================================

class_name BossVisual
extends Node2D

## Bộ điều khiển hiển thị độc lập cho Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Cho phép phân tách hoàn toàn giữa logic FSM, Hitbox và Đồ họa.
## Dễ dàng thay thế bằng SpriteSheet trong tương lai mà không ảnh hưởng AI.

enum VisualState {
	IDLE,
	MOVE,
	TELEGRAPH_CHARGE,
	TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE,
	ATTACK_CHARGE,
	ATTACK_STOMP,
	RECOVERY,
	PHASE_TRANSITION,
	DEFEATED
}

@onready var visual_root: Node2D = $VisualRoot
@onready var body_parts: Node2D = $VisualRoot/BodyParts
@onready var antlers_root: Node2D = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/Antlers
@onready var eye: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/Eye
@onready var lunar_mark: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/BodyParts/Head/LunarMark
@onready var telegraph_charge_rect: ColorRect = $TelegraphCharge
@onready var telegraph_shockwave_ring: ColorRect = $TelegraphShockwave
@onready var aura_rect: ColorRect = $VisualRoot/AuraRect

var current_visual_state: VisualState = VisualState.IDLE
var is_phase_2: bool = false
var _anim_timer: float = 0.0
var _hit_flash_timer: float = 0.0

# Bảng màu Phase 1 (Lam Nguyệt / Cyan Indigo)
const P1_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.12, 0.18, 0.32, 1.0)
const P1_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.25, 0.85, 0.95, 1.0)
const P1_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(0.2, 0.6, 0.9, 0.25)

# Bảng màu Phase 2 (Hắc Nguyệt / Dark Eclipse Violet)
const P2_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.22, 0.08, 0.28, 1.0)
const P2_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.95, 0.2, 0.85, 1.0)
const P2_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(0.8, 0.1, 0.6, 0.35)

# Bảng màu Thanh tẩy thành công (Moonlight Serene)
const CLEANSED_BODY_COLOR: Color = Color(0.88, 0.95, 1.0, 1.0)
const CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR: Color = Color(0.5, 0.9, 1.0, 0.9)
const CLEANSED_AURA_COLOR: Color = Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.6, 0.3)

func _ready() -> void:
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.visible = false
	if telegraph_shockwave_ring:
		telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible = false
	_apply_palette()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_anim_timer += delta

	# Xử lý chớp trắng khi nhận đòn (Hit Flash)
	if _hit_flash_timer > 0.0:
		_hit_flash_timer -= delta
		if _hit_flash_timer <= 0.0:
			_apply_palette()

	# Hoạt ảnh thở / di chuyển / trạng thái
	match current_visual_state:
		VisualState.IDLE, VisualState.RECOVERY:
			var bob: float = sin(_anim_timer * 3.0) * 3.0
			body_parts.position.y = bob
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = sin(_anim_timer * 2.0) * 0.05
		VisualState.MOVE:
			var trot: float = sin(_anim_timer * 10.0) * 5.0
			body_parts.position.y = trot
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = sin(_anim_timer * 10.0) * 0.1
		VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE, VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE:
			# Chớp đỏ cảnh báo
			var pulse: float = (sin(_anim_timer * 16.0) + 1.0) * 0.5
			if telegraph_charge_rect and telegraph_charge_rect.visible:
				telegraph_charge_rect.modulate.a = 0.3 + pulse * 0.4
			if telegraph_shockwave_ring and telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible:
				telegraph_shockwave_ring.modulate.a = 0.3 + pulse * 0.4
		VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION:
			# Rung lắc giải phóng ma lực
			body_parts.position.x = randf_range(-4.0, 4.0)
			body_parts.position.y = randf_range(-4.0, 4.0)
			if aura_rect:
				aura_rect.modulate.a = 0.5 + sin(_anim_timer * 20.0) * 0.3
		VisualState.DEFEATED:
			# Thả lỏng nằm nghỉ ngơi an lành
			body_parts.position.y = 20.0
			body_parts.position.x = 0.0
			if antlers_root:
				antlers_root.rotation = -0.2

func set_facing(dir: int) -> void:
	if visual_root:
		visual_root.scale.x = 1.0 if dir <= 0 else -1.0
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.scale.x = 1.0 if dir <= 0 else -1.0

func set_state(state: VisualState) -> void:
	current_visual_state = state
	if telegraph_charge_rect:
		telegraph_charge_rect.visible = (state == VisualState.TELEGRAPH_CHARGE)
	if telegraph_shockwave_ring:
		telegraph_shockwave_ring.visible = (state == VisualState.TELEGRAPH_SHOCKWAVE)

	if state == VisualState.PHASE_TRANSITION:
		is_phase_2 = true
		_apply_palette()
	elif state == VisualState.DEFEATED:
		_apply_defeated_palette()

func flash_hit() -> void:
	_hit_flash_timer = 0.15
	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(2.5, 2.5, 2.5, 1.0)

func _apply_palette() -> void:
	var body_col: Color = P2_BODY_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_BODY_COLOR
	var antler_col: Color = P2_ANTLER_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_ANTLER_COLOR
	var aura_col: Color = P2_AURA_COLOR if is_phase_2 else P1_AURA_COLOR

	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
		for child in body_parts.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = body_col
	if antlers_root:
		for child in antlers_root.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = antler_col
	if lunar_mark:
		lunar_mark.color = antler_col
	if aura_rect:
		aura_rect.color = aura_col

func _apply_defeated_palette() -> void:
	if body_parts:
		body_parts.modulate = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
		for child in body_parts.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = CLEANSED_BODY_COLOR
	if antlers_root:
		for child in antlers_root.get_children():
			if child is ColorRect:
				child.color = CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR
	if lunar_mark:
		lunar_mark.color = CLEANSED_ANTLER_COLOR
	if aura_rect:
		aura_rect.color = CLEANSED_AURA_COLOR

FILE: src\combat\combat_component.gd
===================================================

class_name CombatComponent
extends Node

signal attack_started()
signal attack_finished()
signal spell_cast()

@export var attack_cooldown: float = 0.35
@export var spell_cooldown: float = 0.6
@export var spell_mana_cost: float = 15.0
@export var melee_hitbox: Hitbox
@export var projectile_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/combat/magic_projectile.tscn")
@export var projectile_spawn_offset: Vector2 = Vector2(30, -40)

var _attack_timer: float = 0.0
var _spell_timer: float = 0.0
var _is_attacking: bool = false
var _attack_anim_duration: float = 0.25

func _ready() -> void:
	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = false
		melee_hitbox.visible = false

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _attack_timer > 0.0:
		_attack_timer -= delta
	if _spell_timer > 0.0:
		_spell_timer -= delta

	if _is_attacking:
		_attack_anim_duration -= delta
		if _attack_anim_duration <= 0.0:
			end_attack()

func can_attack() -> bool:
	return _attack_timer <= 0.0 and not _is_attacking

func can_cast_spell(health_comp: HealthComponent) -> bool:
	if _spell_timer > 0.0 or _is_attacking:
		return false
	if health_comp and health_comp.current_mana < spell_mana_cost:
		return false
	return true

func perform_attack(facing_dir: int) -> bool:
	if not can_attack():
		return false

	_is_attacking = true
	_attack_timer = attack_cooldown
	_attack_anim_duration = 0.22

	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.reset_hits()
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = true
		melee_hitbox.visible = true
		# Căn chỉnh hitbox theo hướng mặt
		melee_hitbox.position.x = abs(melee_hitbox.position.x) * float(facing_dir)

	emit_signal("attack_started")
	return true

func end_attack() -> void:
	_is_attacking = false
	if melee_hitbox:
		melee_hitbox.monitoring = false
		melee_hitbox.visible = false
	emit_signal("attack_finished")

func cast_spell(owner_node: Node2D, facing_dir: int, health_comp: HealthComponent) -> bool:
	if not can_cast_spell(health_comp):
		return false

	if health_comp:
		var success := health_comp.consume_mana(spell_mana_cost)
		if not success:
			return false

	_spell_timer = spell_cooldown

	if projectile_scene and owner_node:
		var proj := projectile_scene.instantiate() as MagicProjectile
		var spawn_pos := owner_node.global_position + Vector2(projectile_spawn_offset.x * float(facing_dir), projectile_spawn_offset.y)
		proj.global_position = spawn_pos
		proj.setup(Vector2(float(facing_dir), 0.0), owner_node)
		var spawn_root: Node = owner_node.get_parent()
		if owner_node.get_tree() and owner_node.get_tree().current_scene:
			spawn_root = owner_node.get_tree().current_scene
		if spawn_root:
			spawn_root.add_child.call_deferred(proj)

	emit_signal("spell_cast")
	return true

func is_attacking() -> bool:
	return _is_attacking

class_name DamageInfo
extends RefCounted

var amount: float = 10.0
var source: Node = null
var knockback_dir: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
var knockback_force: float = 0.0

func _init(p_amount: float = 10.0, p_source: Node = null, p_knockback_dir: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO, p_knockback_force: float = 0.0) -> void:
	amount = p_amount
	source = p_source
	knockback_dir = p_knockback_dir
	knockback_force = p_knockback_force

FILE: src\combat\health_component.gd
===================================================

class_name HealthComponent
extends Node

signal health_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal mana_changed(current: float, max_val: float)
signal damaged(amount: float, source: Node)
signal died()
signal invulnerability_started()
signal invulnerability_ended()

@export var max_health: float = 100.0
@export var current_health: float = 100.0
@export var max_mana: float = 50.0
@export var current_mana: float = 50.0
@export var mana_regen_rate: float = 5.0 # MP hồi phục mỗi giây
@export var invulnerability_duration: float = 0.6 # Thời gian miễn nhiễm sát thương sau khi trúng đòn

var is_invulnerable: bool = false
var _invuln_timer: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	current_health = clamp(current_health, 0.0, max_health)
	current_mana = clamp(current_mana, 0.0, max_mana)
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health, max_health)
	emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Cập nhật thời gian miễn nhiễm sát thương
	if is_invulnerable:
		_invuln_timer -= delta
		if _invuln_timer <= 0.0:
			is_invulnerable = false
			emit_signal("invulnerability_ended")

	# Tự động hồi MP dần theo thời gian nếu chưa đầy
	if mana_regen_rate > 0.0 and current_mana < max_mana:
		current_mana = min(max_mana, current_mana + mana_regen_rate * delta)
		emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)

func take_damage(damage: DamageInfo) -> bool:
	if is_invulnerable or current_health <= 0.0:
		return false

	var final_dmg := damage.amount
	current_health = max(0.0, current_health - final_dmg)
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health, max_health)
	emit_signal("damaged", final_dmg, damage.source)

	if invulnerability_duration > 0.0:
		is_invulnerable = true
		_invuln_timer = invulnerability_duration
		emit_signal("invulnerability_started")

	if current_health <= 0.0:
		emit_signal("died")

	return true

func consume_mana(amount: float) -> bool:
	if current_mana >= amount:
		current_mana -= amount
		emit_signal("mana_changed", current_mana, max_mana)
		return true
	return false

func is_alive() -> bool:
	return current_health > 0.0

FILE: src\combat\hitbox.gd
===================================================

class_name Hitbox
extends Area2D

signal hit_landed(target_hurtbox: Hurtbox)

@export var damage: float = 15.0
@export var knockback_force: float = 200.0
@export var owner_node: Node

var _already_hit_nodes: Array[Node] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	if owner_node == null:
		owner_node = get_parent()
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)

func reset_hits() -> void:
	_already_hit_nodes.clear()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node == owner_node:
			return # Không tự đánh trúng chính mình
		if hurtbox.owner_node in _already_hit_nodes:
			return # Đã trúng trong lượt đánh này rồi

		var knock_dir := Vector2.RIGHT
		if owner_node is Node2D and hurtbox.owner_node is Node2D:
			knock_dir = (hurtbox.owner_node.global_position - owner_node.global_position).normalized()
			if knock_dir == Vector2.ZERO:
				knock_dir = Vector2.RIGHT

		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage, owner_node, knock_dir, knockback_force)
		var success := hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		if success:
			_already_hit_nodes.append(hurtbox.owner_node)
			emit_signal("hit_landed", hurtbox)

class_name Hurtbox
extends Area2D

signal hit_received(damage: DamageInfo)

@export var health_component: HealthComponent
@export var owner_node: Node

func _ready() -> void:
	if owner_node == null:
		owner_node = get_parent()

func apply_damage(damage: DamageInfo) -> bool:
	if health_component and health_component.is_alive():
		var taken = health_component.take_damage(damage)
		if taken:
			emit_signal("hit_received", damage)
		return taken
	return false

FILE: src\combat\magic_projectile.gd

class_name MagicProjectile
extends Area2D

@export var speed: float = 550.0
@export var damage_amount: float = 30.0
@export var lifetime: float = 2.5
@export var knockback_force: float = 250.0

var direction: Vector2 = Vector2.RIGHT
var shooter: Node = null
var _timer: float = 0.0

func setup(p_direction: Vector2, p_shooter: Node, p_damage: float = 30.0) -> void:
	direction = p_direction.normalized()
	shooter = p_shooter
	damage_amount = p_damage
	rotation = direction.angle()

func _ready() -> void:
	area_entered.connect(_on_area_entered)
	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	global_position += direction * speed * delta
	_timer += delta
	if _timer >= lifetime:
		queue_free()

func _on_area_entered(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if area is Hurtbox:
		var hurtbox := area as Hurtbox
		if hurtbox.owner_node == shooter:
			return
		var dmg_info := DamageInfo.new(damage_amount, shooter, direction, knockback_force)
		hurtbox.apply_damage(dmg_info)
		queue_free()

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body == shooter:
		return
	if body is TileMap or body is StaticBody2D:
		queue_free()

class_name InteractionComponent
extends Area2D

signal interaction_available(interactable: Node)
signal interaction_unavailable(interactable: Node)

@export var prompt_text: String = "Nhấn E để nói chuyện"
var is_player_nearby: bool = false
var _nearby_players: Array[Node] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Chỉ nhận Player (Layer 2)
	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
	body_exited.connect(_on_body_exited)

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player:
		if not body in _nearby_players:
			_nearby_players.append(body)
		is_player_nearby = true
		emit_signal("interaction_available", get_parent())

func _on_body_exited(body: Node) -> void:
	if body in _nearby_players:
		_nearby_players.erase(body)
	if _nearby_players.size() == 0:
		is_player_nearby = false
		emit_signal("interaction_unavailable", get_parent())


===================================================
FILE: src\interaction\interaction_coordinator.gd
===================================================

class_name InteractionCoordinator
extends Node

## Bộ điều phối input tập trung cho hệ thống tương tác và hội thoại (M2.1)
## Ngăn chặn race condition giữa NPC và GameUI do thứ tự node trong Scene Tree.

@export var game_ui: GameUI

func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group("interaction_coordinator")
	_ensure_game_ui()

func _ensure_game_ui() -> void:
	if game_ui == null:
		var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur:
			game_ui = cur.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not event.is_action_pressed("interact"):
		return

	_ensure_game_ui()

	# 1. Khi đang mở hội thoại: E chỉ chuyển sang câu tiếp theo hoặc đóng hội thoại
	if game_ui and game_ui.is_dialogue_open():
		game_ui.advance_dialogue()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 2. Khi hội thoại vừa đóng: grace cooldown ngăn chặn nhấn đúp re-open tức thì
	if game_ui and game_ui.is_dialogue_cooling_down():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

	# 3. Khi chưa mở hội thoại: E dùng để kích hoạt tương tác với NPC gần nhất
	var target_npc := _find_nearby_interactable_npc()
	if target_npc:
		target_npc.trigger_interaction()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
		return

func _find_nearby_interactable_npc() -> NPC:
	var npcs = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("npcs")
	for n in npcs:
		if n is NPC and n.is_interactable():
			return n
	return null

FILE: src\inventory\equipment_component.gd
===================================================

class_name EquipmentComponent
extends Node

## Quản lý trang bị của Player (Weapon & Accessory) cho NOCTELYRA (M3)
## Đảm bảo tính toán chỉ số dựa trên Base Stats + Modifiers, không sửa gốc vĩnh viễn.

signal equipment_changed(slot_type: String, item_id: String)

@export var player: Player

var base_attack_damage: float = 25.0
var base_max_mana: float = 60.0

var equipped_weapon: String = ""
var equipped_accessory: String = ""

func _ready() -> void:
	if player == null and get_parent() is Player:
		player = get_parent() as Player

	_record_base_stats()

func _record_base_stats() -> void:
	if player:
		if player.melee_hitbox:
			base_attack_damage = player.melee_hitbox.damage
		var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
		if p_health:
			base_max_mana = p_health.max_mana

func get_equipped(slot_type: String) -> String:
	match slot_type:
		"weapon": return equipped_weapon
		"accessory": return equipped_accessory
		_: return ""

func is_slot_equipped(slot_type: String) -> bool:
	return not get_equipped(slot_type).is_empty()

func equip(item_id: String) -> String:
	if not ItemDatabase.has_item(item_id):
		return ""
	var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
	var prev_item: String = ""

	match def.item_type:
		ItemDefinition.ItemType.WEAPON:
			prev_item = equipped_weapon
			equipped_weapon = item_id
			_recalculate_stats()
			emit_signal("equipment_changed", "weapon", item_id)
		ItemDefinition.ItemType.ACCESSORY:
			prev_item = equipped_accessory
			equipped_accessory = item_id
			_recalculate_stats()
			emit_signal("equipment_changed", "accessory", item_id)
		_:
			return "" # Không phải trang bị

	return prev_item

func unequip(slot_type: String) -> String:
	var old_item: String = ""
	match slot_type:
		"weapon":
			old_item = equipped_weapon
			equipped_weapon = ""
			_recalculate_stats()
			emit_signal("equipment_changed", "weapon", "")
		"accessory":
			old_item = equipped_accessory
			equipped_accessory = ""
			_recalculate_stats()
			emit_signal("equipment_changed", "accessory", "")
		_:
			return ""

	return old_item

func _recalculate_stats() -> void:
	var total_attack := base_attack_damage
	var total_max_mana := base_max_mana

	# Cộng chỉ số từ Weapon
	if not equipped_weapon.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(equipped_weapon):
		var w_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(equipped_weapon)
		total_attack += float(w_def.stat_modifiers.get("attack_bonus", 0.0))

	# Cộng chỉ số từ Accessory
	if not equipped_accessory.is_empty() and ItemDatabase.has_item(equipped_accessory):
		var a_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(equipped_accessory)
		total_max_mana += float(a_def.stat_modifiers.get("max_mana_bonus", 0.0))

	# Áp dụng chỉ số vào Player
	if player:
		if player.melee_hitbox:
			player.melee_hitbox.damage = total_attack

		var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
		if p_health:
			p_health.max_mana = total_max_mana
			# Không tạo MP vượt giới hạn sau khi tháo trang bị
			if p_health.current_mana > total_max_mana:
				p_health.current_mana = total_max_mana
			p_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"weapon": equipped_weapon,
		"accessory": equipped_accessory
	}

func load_from_dict(data: Dictionary) -> void:
	equipped_weapon = str(data.get("weapon", ""))
	equipped_accessory = str(data.get("accessory", ""))

	# Kiểm tra tồn tại trong DB và đúng loại trang bị (M3.1 validation)
	if not ItemDatabase.has_item(equipped_weapon) or ItemDatabase.get_item(equipped_weapon).item_type != ItemDefinition.ItemType.WEAPON:
		equipped_weapon = ""
	if not ItemDatabase.has_item(equipped_accessory) or ItemDatabase.get_item(equipped_accessory).item_type != ItemDefinition.ItemType.ACCESSORY:
		equipped_accessory = ""

	_recalculate_stats()
	emit_signal("equipment_changed", "weapon", equipped_weapon)
	emit_signal("equipment_changed", "accessory", equipped_accessory)


===================================================
FILE: src\inventory\inventory_component.gd
===================================================

class_name InventoryComponent
extends Node

## Quản lý dữ liệu túi đồ (Inventory) 16 ô cho NOCTELYRA (M3)
## Hoàn toàn tách biệt khỏi logic giao diện (UI)

signal inventory_updated()
signal item_added(item_id: String, amount: int)
signal item_removed(item_id: String, amount: int)
signal item_used(item_id: String)

@export var max_slots: int = 16

# Mảng chứa Dictionary các ô đồ: [{ "item_id": String, "amount": int }, ...]
var slots: Array[Dictionary] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	if slots.is_empty():
		_initialize_slots()

func _initialize_slots() -> void:
	slots.clear()
	for i in range(max_slots):
		slots.append({ "item_id": "", "amount": 0 })

func get_slot(index: int) -> Dictionary:
	if index >= 0 and index < slots.size():
		return slots[index]
	return { "item_id": "", "amount": 0 }

func is_slot_empty(index: int) -> bool:
	var s := get_slot(index)
	return str(s.get("item_id", "")).is_empty() or int(s.get("amount", 0)) <= 0

func can_add_item(item_id: String, amount: int = 1) -> bool:
	if amount <= 0 or not ItemDatabase.has_item(item_id):
		return false
	var item_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
	var needed := amount

	# Kiểm tra các ô hiện có cùng loại nếu có thể stack
	if item_def.max_stack > 1:
		for s in slots:
			var s_id: String = str(s.get("item_id", ""))
			var s_amount: int = int(s.get("amount", 0))
			if s_id == item_id and s_amount < item_def.max_stack:
				var space: int = item_def.max_stack - s_amount
				needed -= space
				if needed <= 0:
					return true

	# Kiểm tra các ô trống còn lại
	for s in slots:
		var s_id: String = str(s.get("item_id", ""))
		var s_amount: int = int(s.get("amount", 0))
		if s_id.is_empty() or s_amount <= 0:
			needed -= item_def.max_stack
			if needed <= 0:
				return true

	return false

func add_item(item_id: String, amount: int = 1) -> int:
	if amount <= 0 or not ItemDatabase.has_item(item_id):
		return amount
	var item_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
	var remaining := amount

	# 1. Điền vào các ô cùng loại chưa đầy nếu stack được
	if item_def.max_stack > 1:
		for i in range(slots.size()):
			if remaining <= 0:
				break
			var s := slots[i]
			if s.item_id == item_id and s.amount < item_def.max_stack:
				var can_take: int = min(remaining, item_def.max_stack - s.amount)
				s.amount += can_take
				remaining -= can_take

	# 2. Điền vào các ô trống mới
	for i in range(slots.size()):
		if remaining <= 0:
			break
		var s := slots[i]
		if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
			var can_take: int = min(remaining, item_def.max_stack)
			s.item_id = item_id
			s.amount = can_take
			remaining -= can_take

	var added_total: int = amount - remaining
	if added_total > 0:
		emit_signal("item_added", item_id, added_total)
		emit_signal("inventory_updated")

	return remaining

func remove_item_at(slot_idx: int, amount: int = 1) -> bool:
	if slot_idx < 0 or slot_idx >= slots.size() or amount <= 0:
		return false
	var s := slots[slot_idx]
	if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
		return false

	var item_id: String = s.item_id
	var to_remove: int = min(amount, s.amount)
	s.amount -= to_remove

	if s.amount <= 0:
		s.item_id = ""
		s.amount = 0

	emit_signal("item_removed", item_id, to_remove)
	emit_signal("inventory_updated")
	return true

func remove_item_by_id(item_id: String, amount: int = 1) -> bool:
	if amount <= 0 or not has_item(item_id, amount):
		return false
	var remaining := amount
	for i in range(slots.size() - 1, -1, -1):
		if remaining <= 0:
			break
		var s := slots[i]
		if s.item_id == item_id:
			var to_remove: int = min(remaining, s.amount)
			s.amount -= to_remove
			remaining -= to_remove
			if s.amount <= 0:
				s.item_id = ""
				s.amount = 0

	emit_signal("item_removed", item_id, amount)
	emit_signal("inventory_updated")
	return true

func has_item(item_id: String, amount: int = 1) -> bool:
	var count := get_item_count(item_id)
	return count >= amount

func get_item_count(item_id: String) -> int:
	var count: int = 0
	for s in slots:
		if s.item_id == item_id:
			count += s.amount
	return count

func use_item_at(slot_idx: int, player: Player) -> bool:
	if slot_idx < 0 or slot_idx >= slots.size() or player == null:
		return false
	var s := slots[slot_idx]
	if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
		return false

	var item_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(s.item_id)
	if item_def == null or item_def.item_type != ItemDefinition.ItemType.CONSUMABLE:
		return false

	# Kiểm tra trạng thái sống của Player
	var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
	if p_health == null or not p_health.is_alive():
		return false # Không thể sử dụng bình khi đã chết

	# Áp dụng hiệu ứng
	var used := false
	if item_def.use_effect.has("heal_hp"):
		var heal_val: float = float(item_def.use_effect["heal_hp"])
		p_health.current_health = min(p_health.max_health, p_health.current_health + heal_val)
		p_health.emit_signal("health_changed", p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
		used = true

	if used:
		var item_id: String = s.item_id
		remove_item_at(slot_idx, 1)
		emit_signal("item_used", item_id)
		return true

	return false

func to_dict() -> Array:
	var arr: Array = []
	for s in slots:
		arr.append({
			"item_id": s.item_id,
			"amount": s.amount
		})
	return arr

func load_from_dict(arr: Array) -> void:
	_initialize_slots()
	for i in range(min(arr.size(), max_slots)):
		var entry = arr[i]
		if entry is Dictionary:
			var id: String = str(entry.get("item_id", ""))
			var amt: int = int(entry.get("amount", 0))
			if not id.is_empty() and amt > 0 and ItemDatabase.has_item(id):
				var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(id)
				slots[i].item_id = id
				slots[i].amount = clamp(amt, 1, def.max_stack)
			else:
				slots[i].item_id = ""
				slots[i].amount = 0
	emit_signal("inventory_updated")


===================================================
FILE: src\inventory\inventory_ui.gd
===================================================

class_name InventoryUI
extends Control

## Giao diện Túi đồ (Inventory UI) 16 ô và 2 ô trang bị cho NOCTELYRA (M3)

signal inventory_opened()
signal inventory_closed()

@export var player: Player

@onready var grid_container: GridContainer = $Panel/MainHBox/LeftVBox/GridContainer
@onready var weapon_slot_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/LeftVBox/EquipHBox/WeaponSlotBtn
@onready var accessory_slot_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/LeftVBox/EquipHBox/AccessorySlotBtn
@onready var close_btn: Button = $Panel/TitleHBox/CloseBtn

# Bảng chi tiết vật phẩm
@onready var detail_icon: TextureRect = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/DetailIcon
@onready var detail_name: Label = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/DetailName
@onready var detail_type: Label = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/DetailType
@onready var detail_desc: Label = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/DetailDesc
@onready var detail_stats: Label = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/DetailStats

# Nút hành động
@onready var use_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/ActionHBox/UseBtn
@onready var equip_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/ActionHBox/EquipBtn
@onready var unequip_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/ActionHBox/UnequipBtn
@onready var drop_btn: Button = $Panel/MainHBox/RightVBox/ActionHBox/DropBtn

var _selected_slot_idx: int = -1
var _selected_equip_slot: String = "" # "weapon", "accessory", hoặc ""
var _slot_buttons: Array[Button] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	visible = false
	if close_btn:
		close_btn.pressed.connect(close)
	if use_btn:
		use_btn.pressed.connect(_on_use_pressed)
	if equip_btn:
		equip_btn.pressed.connect(_on_equip_pressed)
	if unequip_btn:
		unequip_btn.pressed.connect(_on_unequip_pressed)
	if drop_btn:
		drop_btn.pressed.connect(_on_drop_pressed)

	if weapon_slot_btn:
		weapon_slot_btn.pressed.connect(func(): _select_equip_slot("weapon"))
	if accessory_slot_btn:
		accessory_slot_btn.pressed.connect(func(): _select_equip_slot("accessory"))

	_setup_slots()
	_clear_details()

func setup_player(p: Player) -> void:
	player = p
	if player:
		var inv := player.inventory_component
		if inv and not inv.inventory_updated.is_connected(refresh):
			inv.inventory_updated.connect(refresh)
		var eq := player.equipment_component
		if eq and not eq.equipment_changed.is_connected(func(_st, _id): refresh()):
			eq.equipment_changed.connect(func(_st, _id): refresh())
	refresh()

func _ensure_player() -> void:
	if player == null:
		var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur:
			var p = cur.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
			if p:
				setup_player(p)

func _setup_slots() -> void:
	if grid_container == null:
		return
	_slot_buttons.clear()
	for child in grid_container.get_children():
		child.queue_free()

	for i in range(16):
		var btn := Button.new()
		btn.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(56, 56)
		btn.clip_contents = true

		var icon := TextureRect.new()
		icon.name = "Icon"
		icon.expand_mode = TextureRect.EXPAND_IGNORE_SIZE
		icon.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_CENTERED
		icon.anchor_right = 1.0
		icon.anchor_bottom = 1.0
		icon.offset_left = 4
		icon.offset_top = 4
		icon.offset_right = -4
		icon.offset_bottom = -4
		btn.add_child(icon)

		var lbl := Label.new()
		lbl.name = "Count"
		lbl.anchor_right = 1.0
		lbl.anchor_bottom = 1.0
		lbl.offset_right = -4
		lbl.offset_bottom = -2
		lbl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
		lbl.vertical_alignment = VERTICAL_ALIGNMENT_BOTTOM
		lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
		btn.add_child(lbl)

		var slot_idx := i
		btn.pressed.connect(func(): _select_slot(slot_idx))
		grid_container.add_child(btn)
		_slot_buttons.append(btn)

func open() -> void:
	_ensure_player()
	visible = true
	refresh()
	emit_signal("inventory_opened")

func close() -> void:
	visible = false
	_clear_details()
	emit_signal("inventory_closed")

func toggle() -> void:
	if visible:
		close()
	else:
		open()

func is_open() -> bool:
	return visible

func refresh() -> void:
	_ensure_player()
	if player == null:
		return

	var inv := player.inventory_component
	var eq := player.equipment_component

	# 1. Cập nhật 16 ô đồ
	if inv:
		for i in range(min(_slot_buttons.size(), inv.slots.size())):
			var btn := _slot_buttons[i]
			var icon := btn.get_node_or_null("Icon") as TextureRect
			var lbl := btn.get_node_or_null("Count") as Label
			var s := inv.get_slot(i)

			if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
				if icon: icon.texture = null
				if lbl: lbl.text = ""
			else:
				var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(s.item_id)
				if icon and def:
					icon.texture = def.icon
				if lbl:
					lbl.text = str(s.amount) if s.amount > 1 else ""

	# 2. Cập nhật 2 ô trang bị
	if eq:
		if weapon_slot_btn:
			var w_id := eq.get_equipped("weapon")
			if w_id.is_empty():
				weapon_slot_btn.text = "Vũ khí: Trống"
				weapon_slot_btn.icon = null
			else:
				var w_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(w_id)
				weapon_slot_btn.text = "VK: " + (w_def.display_name if w_def else w_id)
				weapon_slot_btn.icon = w_def.icon if w_def else null

		if accessory_slot_btn:
			var a_id := eq.get_equipped("accessory")
			if a_id.is_empty():
				accessory_slot_btn.text = "Phụ kiện: Trống"
				accessory_slot_btn.icon = null
			else:
				var a_def := ItemDatabase.get_item(a_id)
				accessory_slot_btn.text = "PK: " + (a_def.display_name if a_def else a_id)
				accessory_slot_btn.icon = a_def.icon if a_def else null

	# Làm mới hiển thị chi tiết vật phẩm đang chọn
	if _selected_slot_idx >= 0:
		_select_slot(_selected_slot_idx)
	elif not _selected_equip_slot.is_empty():
		_select_equip_slot(_selected_equip_slot)
	else:
		_clear_details()

func _select_slot(index: int) -> void:
	_selected_slot_idx = index
	_selected_equip_slot = ""
	_ensure_player()

	if player == null or player.inventory_component == null:
		_clear_details()
		return

	var s := player.inventory_component.get_slot(index)
	if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
		_clear_details()
		return

	var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(s.item_id)
	if def == null:
		_clear_details()
		return

	_display_item_details(def, s.amount, false)

func _select_equip_slot(slot_type: String) -> void:
	_selected_slot_idx = -1
	_selected_equip_slot = slot_type
	_ensure_player()

	if player == null or player.equipment_component == null:
		_clear_details()
		return

	var item_id := player.equipment_component.get_equipped(slot_type)
	if item_id.is_empty():
		_clear_details()
		detail_name.text = "Ô trang bị %s (Đang trống)" % ("Vũ khí" if slot_type == "weapon" else "Phụ kiện")
		return

	var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
	if def:
		_display_item_details(def, 1, true)

func _display_item_details(def: ItemDefinition, amount: int, is_equipped_slot: bool) -> void:
	if detail_icon:
		detail_icon.texture = def.icon
	if detail_name:
		detail_name.text = "%s (%d)" % [def.display_name, amount] if amount > 1 else def.display_name
	if detail_type:
		detail_type.text = "Loại: %s | Giá trị: %d G" % [def.get_type_name(), def.sell_value]
	if detail_desc:
		detail_desc.text = def.description

	var stat_text := ""
	if def.item_type == ItemDefinition.ItemType.WEAPON:
		stat_text = "+%d Sát thương đánh thường" % int(def.stat_modifiers.get("attack_bonus", 0))
	elif def.item_type == ItemDefinition.ItemType.ACCESSORY:
		stat_text = "+%d Max MP" % int(def.stat_modifiers.get("max_mana_bonus", 0))
	elif def.item_type == ItemDefinition.ItemType.CONSUMABLE:
		stat_text = "Hồi %d HP" % int(def.use_effect.get("heal_hp", 0))
	if detail_stats:
		detail_stats.text = stat_text

	# Cập nhật trạng thái các nút
	if is_equipped_slot:
		if use_btn: use_btn.disabled = true
		if equip_btn: equip_btn.disabled = true
		if unequip_btn: unequip_btn.disabled = false
		if drop_btn: drop_btn.disabled = true
	else:
		if use_btn:
			use_btn.disabled = (def.item_type != ItemDefinition.ItemType.CONSUMABLE)
		if equip_btn:
			equip_btn.disabled = not (def.item_type == ItemDefinition.ItemType.WEAPON or def.item_type == ItemDefinition.ItemType.ACCESSORY)
		if unequip_btn:
			unequip_btn.disabled = true
		if drop_btn:
			drop_btn.disabled = false

func _clear_details() -> void:
	if detail_icon: detail_icon.texture = null
	if detail_name: detail_name.text = "Chọn một vật phẩm để xem chi tiết"
	if detail_type: detail_type.text = ""
	if detail_desc: detail_desc.text = ""
	if detail_stats: detail_stats.text = ""
	if use_btn: use_btn.disabled = true
	if equip_btn: equip_btn.disabled = true
	if unequip_btn: unequip_btn.disabled = true
	if drop_btn: drop_btn.disabled = true

func _on_use_pressed() -> void:
	if _selected_slot_idx < 0 or player == null or player.inventory_component == null:
		return
	player.inventory_component.use_item_at(_selected_slot_idx, player)
	refresh()

func _on_equip_pressed() -> void:
	if _selected_slot_idx < 0 or player == null:
		return
	var inv := player.inventory_component
	var eq := player.equipment_component
	if inv == null or eq == null:
		return

	var s := inv.get_slot(_selected_slot_idx)
	if s.item_id.is_empty():
		return

	var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(s.item_id)
	if def == null:
		return

	var item_to_equip: String = s.item_id
	var prev_item := eq.equip(item_to_equip)
	inv.remove_item_at(_selected_slot_idx, 1)

	if not prev_item.is_empty():
		inv.add_item(prev_item, 1)

	refresh()

func _on_unequip_pressed() -> void:
	if _selected_equip_slot.is_empty() or player == null:
		return
	var inv := player.inventory_component
	var eq := player.equipment_component
	if inv == null or eq == null:
		return

	var item_id := eq.get_equipped(_selected_equip_slot)
	if item_id.is_empty():
		return

	if not inv.can_add_item(item_id, 1):
		var ui = get_parent() as GameUI
		if ui:
			ui.show_notification("Túi đồ đã đầy! Không thể tháo trang bị.")
		return

	var unequipped := eq.unequip(_selected_equip_slot)
	if not unequipped.is_empty():
		inv.add_item(unequipped, 1)

	refresh()

func _on_drop_pressed() -> void:
	if _selected_slot_idx < 0 or player == null:
		return
	var inv := player.inventory_component
	if inv == null:
		return

	var s := inv.get_slot(_selected_slot_idx)
	if s.item_id.is_empty() or s.amount <= 0:
		return

	var drop_id: String = s.item_id
	inv.remove_item_at(_selected_slot_idx, 1)

	# Sinh ItemPickup ra thế giới
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	var pickup := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
	pickup.item_id = drop_id
	pickup.amount = 1
	var drop_pos := player.global_position + Vector2(float(player.facing_direction) * 45.0, 0.0)
	pickup.global_position = drop_pos
	if player.get_parent():
		player.get_parent().add_child(pickup)

	refresh()

FILE: src\inventory\item_database.gd

class_name ItemDatabase
extends RefCounted

## Cơ sở dữ liệu vật phẩm (ItemRegistry) cho NOCTELYRA
## Cho phép tra cứu ItemDefinition ổn định theo item_id.

static var _items: Dictionary = {}
static var _is_initialized: bool = false

static func initialize() -> void:
	if _is_initialized:
		return
	_items.clear()

	# 1. Tinh thể Ma lực (mana_shard)
	var mana_shard = ItemDefinition.new()
	mana_shard.item_id = "mana_shard"
	mana_shard.display_name = "Tinh thể Ma lực"
	mana_shard.description = "Mảnh vỡ ngưng tụ ma lực bóng đêm rơi ra từ sinh vật tha hóa. Dùng làm nguyên liệu."
	mana_shard.item_type = ItemDefinition.ItemType.MATERIAL
	mana_shard.max_stack = 99
	mana_shard.sell_value = 5
	mana_shard.stat_modifiers = {}
	mana_shard.use_effect = {}
	mana_shard.icon = _create_placeholder_icon(Color(0.6, 0.2, 0.9, 1.0)) # Tím ma thuật
	register_item(mana_shard)

	# 2. Bình Sinh Lực (health_potion)
	var potion = ItemDefinition.new()
	potion.item_id = "health_potion"
	potion.display_name = "Bình Sinh Lực"
	potion.description = "Dược tễ thảo mộc ánh nguyệt, hồi phục ngay lập tức 30 HP (không vượt quá Max HP)."
	potion.item_type = ItemDefinition.ItemType.CONSUMABLE
	potion.max_stack = 20
	potion.sell_value = 15
	potion.stat_modifiers = {}
	potion.use_effect = {"heal_hp": 30.0}
	potion.icon = _create_placeholder_icon(Color(0.9, 0.2, 0.2, 1.0)) # Đỏ sinh mệnh
	register_item(potion)

	# 3. Kiếm Ánh Trăng (moonsteel_sword)
	var sword = ItemDefinition.new()
	sword.item_id = "moonsteel_sword"
	sword.display_name = "Kiếm Ánh Trăng"
	sword.description = "Thanh kiếm rèn từ thép ánh nguyệt tinh khiết. Khi trang bị, cộng thêm 10 sát thương đánh thường."
	sword.item_type = ItemDefinition.ItemType.WEAPON
	sword.max_stack = 1
	sword.sell_value = 100
	sword.stat_modifiers = {"attack_bonus": 10.0}
	sword.use_effect = {}
	sword.icon = _create_placeholder_icon(Color(0.4, 0.8, 1.0, 1.0)) # Xanh ánh thép
	register_item(sword)

	# 4. Bùa Nguyệt Quang (moon_amulet)
	var amulet = ItemDefinition.new()
	amulet.item_id = "moon_amulet"
	amulet.display_name = "Bùa Nguyệt Quang"
	amulet.description = "Dây chuyền khắc cổ ngữ bảo hộ của Làng Ánh Nguyệt. Khi trang bị, cộng thêm 20 Max MP."
	amulet.item_type = ItemDefinition.ItemType.ACCESSORY
	amulet.max_stack = 1
	amulet.sell_value = 120
	amulet.stat_modifiers = {"max_mana_bonus": 20.0}
	amulet.use_effect = {}
	amulet.icon = _create_placeholder_icon(Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.3, 1.0)) # Vàng ánh trăng
	register_item(amulet)

	# 5. Mảnh Nguyệt Thực (eclipse_fragment) - [MỚI M4: Phần thưởng Boss Lunareth]
	var fragment = ItemDefinition.new()
	fragment.item_id = "eclipse_fragment"
	fragment.display_name = "Mảnh Nguyệt Thực"
	fragment.description = "Mảnh tinh thể ngưng tụ từ ma lực hắc nguyệt đã được thanh tẩy của linh thú Lunareth. Cổ vật ma đạo quý hiếm."
	fragment.item_type = ItemDefinition.ItemType.MATERIAL
	fragment.max_stack = 99
	fragment.sell_value = 250
	fragment.stat_modifiers = {}
	fragment.use_effect = {}
	fragment.icon = _create_placeholder_icon(Color(0.85, 0.35, 0.95, 1.0)) # Tím nguyệt thực rực rỡ
	register_item(fragment)

	_is_initialized = true

static func register_item(item: ItemDefinition) -> void:
	if item and not item.item_id.is_empty():
		_items[item.item_id] = item

static func get_item(id: String) -> ItemDefinition:
	if not _is_initialized:
		initialize()
	return _items.get(id, null)

static func has_item(id: String) -> bool:
	if not _is_initialized:
		initialize()
	return _items.has(id)

static func get_all_items() -> Array[ItemDefinition]:
	if not _is_initialized:
		initialize()
	var arr: Array[ItemDefinition] = []
	for k in _items.keys():
		arr.append(_items[k])
	return arr

static func _create_placeholder_icon(base_color: Color) -> ImageTexture:
	var img := Image.create(32, 32, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.fill(Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.16, 1.0))
	for y in range(4, 28):
		for x in range(4, 28):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, base_color)
	for y in range(8, 24):
		for x in range(8, 24):
			var light_col := base_color.lightened(0.3)
			img.set_pixel(x, y, light_col)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)

class_name ItemDefinition
extends Resource

## Định nghĩa vật phẩm chuẩn cho NOCTELYRA (Milestone M3)

enum ItemType {
	MATERIAL,
	CONSUMABLE,
	WEAPON,
	ACCESSORY
}

@export var item_id: String = ""
@export var display_name: String = ""
@export_multiline var description: String = ""
@export var item_type: ItemType = ItemType.MATERIAL
@export var icon: Texture2D = null
@export var max_stack: int = 99
@export var sell_value: int = 0
@export var stat_modifiers: Dictionary = {}
@export var use_effect: Dictionary = {}

func get_type_name() -> String:
	match item_type:
		ItemType.MATERIAL: return "Nguyên liệu"
		ItemType.CONSUMABLE: return "Tiêu hao"
		ItemType.WEAPON: return "Vũ khí"
		ItemType.ACCESSORY: return "Phụ kiện"
		_: return "Khác"

class_name ItemPickup
extends Area2D

## Vật phẩm rơi trên mặt đất (Loot Pickup) cho NOCTELYRA (M3.1)
## Hỗ trợ pickup_id ổn định cho vật phẩm đặt sẵn và khôi phục vật phẩm rơi động.

@export var item_id: String = "mana_shard"
@export var amount: int = 1
@export var pickup_id: String = ""
@export var is_dynamic: bool = false

@onready var icon_sprite: Sprite2D = $IconSprite
@onready var label: Label = $Label
@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D

var _base_y: float = 0.0
var _anim_time: float = 0.0
var is_picked_up: bool = false

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 0
	collision_mask = 2 # Player trên Layer 2
	_base_y = position.y
	_update_visuals()
	body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)

	if is_dynamic:
		add_to_group("dynamic_pickups")
	else:
		add_to_group("preplaced_pickups")
		# Nếu là vật phẩm đặt sẵn đã được lưu là đã nhặt thì tự hủy
		var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur_scene and "collected_pickup_ids" in cur_scene:
			if pickup_id in cur_scene.collected_pickup_ids:
				queue_free()

func setup(p_item_id: String, p_amount: int = 1) -> void:
	item_id = p_item_id
	amount = p_amount
	_update_visuals()

func _update_visuals() -> void:
	var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
	if def:
		if label:
			if amount > 1:
				label.text = "%s x%d" % [def.display_name, amount]
			else:
				label.text = def.display_name
		if icon_sprite and def.icon:
			icon_sprite.texture = def.icon

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Hiệu ứng trôi nổi nhẹ nhàng (bobbing effect)
	_anim_time += delta * 3.5
	position.y = _base_y + sin(_anim_time) * 4.0

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if is_picked_up:
		return

	if body is Player:
		var inv := body.get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
		if inv == null:
			return

		# Kiểm tra xem túi đồ có đủ chỗ không
		if inv.can_add_item(item_id, amount):
			is_picked_up = true
			var left := inv.add_item(item_id, amount)
			var def := ItemDatabase.get_item(item_id)
			var item_name := def.display_name if def else item_id

			# Ghi nhận ID đã nhặt nếu là vật phẩm đặt sẵn
			if not is_dynamic and not pickup_id.is_empty():
				var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
				if cur_scene and cur_scene.has_method("register_collected_pickup"):
					cur_scene.register_collected_pickup(pickup_id)

			# Thông báo nhặt đồ lên HUD
			var ui := _get_game_ui()
			if ui:
				ui.show_notification("Đã nhặt: %s x%d" % [item_name, amount - left])

			queue_free()
		else:
			# Túi đồ đầy, không nhặt và giữ nguyên trên mặt đất
			var ui := _get_game_ui()
			if ui:
				ui.show_notification("Túi đồ đã đầy! Không thể nhặt thêm.")

func _get_game_ui() -> GameUI:
	var cur = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
	if cur:
		return cur.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	return null

class_name LootTable
extends Resource

## Bảng rơi vật phẩm (Loot Table) cho Enemy trong NOCTELYRA (M3)
## Tách biệt hoàn toàn khỏi logic AI của Enemy.

# Mảng các quy tắc rơi: [{ "item_id": String, "chance": float, "min_amount": int, "max_amount": int }]
@export var drop_entries: Array[Dictionary] = [
	{
		"item_id": "mana_shard",
		"chance": 1.0, # 100% tỷ lệ rơi để kiểm thử nghiệm thu M3
		"min_amount": 1,
		"max_amount": 1
	}
]

func roll_drops() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var result: Array[Dictionary] = []
	for entry in drop_entries:
		var chance: float = float(entry.get("chance", 1.0))
		if randf() <= chance:
			var min_amt: int = int(entry.get("min_amount", 1))
			var max_amt: int = int(entry.get("max_amount", 1))
			var amt := randi_range(min_amt, max_amt)
			if amt > 0:
				result.append({
					"item_id": str(entry.get("item_id", "mana_shard")),
					"amount": amt
				})
	return result

func spawn_loot(pos: Vector2, parent_node: Node) -> void:
	if parent_node == null:
		return
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	var items := roll_drops()
	for i in range(items.size()):
		var item_info := items[i]
		var pickup := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
		pickup.item_id = item_info["item_id"]
		pickup.amount = item_info["amount"]
		pickup.is_dynamic = true
		pickup.pickup_id = "drop_" + str(Time.get_ticks_usec()) + "_" + str(randi() % 100000)
		pickup.global_position = pos + Vector2(float(i * 24 - (items.size() - 1) * 12), 0.0)
		parent_node.add_child.call_deferred(pickup)

class_name QuestData
extends Resource

enum State {
	NOT_STARTED,
	ACTIVE,
	READY_TO_TURN_IN,
	COMPLETED
}

@export var id: String = "light_swallowed"
@export var title: String = "Ánh Sáng Bị Nuốt Chửng"
@export var description: String = "Ma lực tại Rừng Hắc Nguyệt đang mất cân bằng. Hãy tiêu diệt 3 sinh vật bị ma lực tha hóa để bảo vệ ngôi làng."
@export var target_amount: int = 3
@export var current_amount: int = 0
@export var reward_gold: int = 50
@export var current_state: State = State.NOT_STARTED


===================================================
FILE: src\quest\quest_manager.gd
===================================================

class_name QuestManager
extends Node

signal quest_started(quest: QuestData)
signal quest_progress_updated(quest: QuestData)
signal quest_ready_to_turn_in(quest: QuestData)
signal quest_completed(quest: QuestData)
signal gold_changed(new_gold: int)
signal notification_posted(message: String)

@export var current_quest: QuestData
var gold: int = 0
var _killed_enemy_ids: Array[String] = []

func _ready() -> void:
	if current_quest == null:
		current_quest = QuestData.new()

func start_quest() -> bool:
	if current_quest == null or current_quest.current_state != QuestData.State.NOT_STARTED:
		return false
	current_quest.current_state = QuestData.State.ACTIVE
	current_quest.current_amount = 0
	_killed_enemy_ids.clear()
	emit_signal("quest_started", current_quest)
	emit_signal("notification_posted", "Đã nhận nhiệm vụ: %s" % current_quest.title)
	return true

func register_enemy_kill(enemy: Node) -> void:
	if current_quest == null or current_quest.current_state != QuestData.State.ACTIVE:
		return
	
	var kill_id: String = ""
	if enemy != null and "enemy_id" in enemy and not str(enemy.enemy_id).is_empty():
		kill_id = str(enemy.enemy_id)
	elif enemy != null:
		kill_id = "inst_" + str(enemy.get_instance_id())
	else:
		return

	if kill_id in _killed_enemy_ids:
		return # Đảm bảo mỗi quái chỉ tính 1 lần theo ID ổn định
	
	_killed_enemy_ids.append(kill_id)
	current_quest.current_amount += 1
	emit_signal("quest_progress_updated", current_quest)
	emit_signal("notification_posted", "Tiến độ: %d/%d sinh vật tha hóa" % [current_quest.current_amount, current_quest.target_amount])

	if current_quest.current_amount >= current_quest.target_amount:
		current_quest.current_state = QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN
		emit_signal("quest_ready_to_turn_in", current_quest)
		emit_signal("notification_posted", "Nhiệm vụ hoàn tất! Hãy về gặp Elyra để nhận thưởng.")

func complete_quest(player_health: HealthComponent = null) -> bool:
	if current_quest == null or current_quest.current_state != QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
		return false
	
	current_quest.current_state = QuestData.State.COMPLETED
	gold += current_quest.reward_gold
	emit_signal("gold_changed", gold)

	if player_health:
		player_health.current_health = player_health.max_health
		player_health.current_mana = player_health.max_mana
		player_health.emit_signal("health_changed", player_health.current_health, player_health.max_health)
		player_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", player_health.current_mana, player_health.max_mana)

	emit_signal("quest_completed", current_quest)
	emit_signal("notification_posted", "Hoàn thành: Nhận %d Vàng & hồi đầy HP/MP!" % current_quest.reward_gold)
	return true


===================================================
FILE: src\save\save_manager.gd
===================================================

class_name SaveManager
extends RefCounted

## Hệ thống Lưu & Tải Tiến trình (Save/Load System) cho NOCTELYRA (M3.2 FINAL)
## Hỗ trợ ghi tạm nguyên tử (Atomic write), backup và rollback khi lỗi,
## nạp trang bị trước để tính toán chính xác Max MP/Current MP,
## thẩm định (validation) chặt chẽ và cách ly dữ liệu kiểm thử an toàn 100%.

const DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH: String = "user://noctelyra_slot1.json"
const CURRENT_VERSION: String = "1.1.0"
const SUPPORTED_VERSIONS: Array[String] = ["1.0.0", "1.1.0"]

static var custom_save_path: String = ""
static var should_load_on_start: bool = false

# Các cờ mô phỏng lỗi dùng riêng cho kiểm thử an toàn ghi file (Fault Injection)
static var simulate_tmp_write_error: bool = false
static var simulate_backup_error: bool = false
static var simulate_replace_error: bool = false

static func get_save_path() -> String:
	if not custom_save_path.is_empty():
		return custom_save_path
	return DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH

static func get_tmp_path() -> String:
	return get_save_path() + ".tmp"

static func get_bak_path() -> String:
	return get_save_path() + ".bak"

static func has_save() -> bool:
	var path := get_save_path()
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return false
	var file := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return false
	var text := file.get_as_text()
	file.close()

	if text.strip_edges().is_empty():
		return false

	var test_json := JSON.new()
	var err := test_json.parse(text)
	if err != OK or not (test_json.data is Dictionary):
		return false

	var dict: Dictionary = test_json.data

	# Thẩm định phiên bản save_version
	if not dict.has("save_version") or not (dict["save_version"] is String):
		return false
	var ver: String = dict["save_version"]
	if not ver in SUPPORTED_VERSIONS:
		return false

	# Thẩm định cấu trúc dữ liệu cốt lõi bắt buộc phải tồn tại
	if not dict.has("player") or not (dict["player"] is Dictionary):
		return false
	if not dict.has("quest") or not (dict["quest"] is Dictionary):
		return false

	return true

static func delete_save() -> bool:
	var path := get_save_path()
	var success := true
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		var err := DirAccess.remove_absolute(path)
		if err != OK: success = false
	var tmp_path := get_tmp_path()
	if FileAccess.file_exists(tmp_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
	var bak_path := get_bak_path()
	if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)
	return success

static func save_game(world: Node) -> bool:
	if world == null:
		return false

	var player := world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr := world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	var ui := world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

	if player == null or quest_mgr == null:
		return false

	var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
	var p_inv := player.get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
	var p_eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent

	var save_path := get_save_path()
	var tmp_path := get_tmp_path()
	var bak_path := get_bak_path()

	var save_data: Dictionary = {
		"save_version": CURRENT_VERSION,
		"timestamp": Time.get_unix_time_from_system(),
		"player": {
			"position_x": player.global_position.x,
			"position_y": player.global_position.y,
			"current_health": clamp(p_health.current_health, 0.0, p_health.max_health) if p_health else 100.0,
			"current_mana": clamp(p_health.current_mana, 0.0, p_health.max_mana) if p_health else 60.0
		},
		"gold": max(0, quest_mgr.gold),
		"inventory": p_inv.to_dict() if p_inv else [],
		"equipment": p_eq.to_dict() if p_eq else {},
		"quest": {
			"state": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state) if quest_mgr.current_quest else 0,
			"current_amount": int(quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount) if quest_mgr.current_quest else 0
		},
		"defeated_enemy_ids": world.get("defeated_enemy_ids") if "defeated_enemy_ids" in world else [],
		"collected_pickup_ids": world.get("collected_pickup_ids") if "collected_pickup_ids" in world else [],
		"dropped_pickups": world.get_dropped_pickups_data() if world.has_method("get_dropped_pickups_data") else [],
		"boss": {
			"activated": world.get("boss_battle_started") if "boss_battle_started" in world else false,
			"defeated": world.get("boss_defeated") if "boss_defeated" in world else false,
			"reward_claimed": world.get("boss_reward_claimed") if "boss_reward_claimed" in world else false
		}
	}

	var json_str := JSON.stringify(save_data, "\t")

	# 1. Ghi an toàn vào file tạm .tmp (Kiểm tra fault injection)
	if simulate_tmp_write_error:
		push_error("[Mô phỏng lỗi]: Thất bại khi ghi file tạm!")
		return false

	var tmp_file := FileAccess.open(tmp_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if tmp_file == null:
		push_error("Không thể mở file tạm để ghi: " + tmp_path)
		return false

	tmp_file.store_string(json_str)
	tmp_file.flush()
	tmp_file.close()

	# 2. Xác minh dữ liệu trong file tạm trước khi chạm vào file cũ
	var verify_file := FileAccess.open(tmp_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if verify_file == null:
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
		return false
	var verify_text := verify_file.get_as_text()
	verify_file.close()

	var verify_json := JSON.new()
	if verify_json.parse(verify_text) != OK or not (verify_json.data is Dictionary):
		push_error("Xác minh file tạm thất bại! Giữ nguyên bản lưu cũ.")
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
		return false

	# 3. Tạo bản sao lưu dự phòng .bak từ file hiện tại (nếu đã có file save cũ)
	var has_old := FileAccess.file_exists(save_path)
	if has_old:
		if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
			DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)

		if simulate_backup_error:
			push_error("[Mô phỏng lỗi]: Thất bại ở bước tạo bản sao lưu .bak!")
			DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
			return false

		var copy_bak_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(save_path, bak_path)
		if copy_bak_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
			var bak_err := DirAccess.rename_absolute(save_path, bak_path)
			if bak_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
				push_error("Không thể tạo bản sao lưu backup!")
				DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
				return false

	# 4. Thay thế an toàn file chính bằng file tạm (.tmp -> save_path)
	var replace_err := DirAccess.rename_absolute(tmp_path, save_path)
	if replace_err != OK or simulate_replace_error:
		var copy_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(tmp_path, save_path)
		if copy_err != OK or simulate_replace_error:
			push_error("Lỗi khi thay thế file save! Phục hồi bản lưu cũ từ backup.")
			if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
				var restore_err := DirAccess.copy_absolute(bak_path, save_path)
				if restore_err != OK and not FileAccess.file_exists(save_path):
					DirAccess.rename_absolute(bak_path, save_path)
				if FileAccess.file_exists(save_path) and FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
					DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)
			if FileAccess.file_exists(tmp_path):
				DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)
			return false
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(tmp_path)

	# 5. Hoàn tất thành công, dọn dẹp file backup
	if FileAccess.file_exists(bak_path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(bak_path)

	if ui:
		ui.show_notification("Đã lưu tiến trình game thành công!")

	return true

static func load_game(world: Node) -> bool:
	if world == null or not has_save():
		return false

	var player := world.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	var quest_mgr := world.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	var ui := world.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI

	if player == null or quest_mgr == null:
		push_error("load_game thất bại: World thiếu Player hoặc QuestManager.")
		return false

	var save_path := get_save_path()
	var file := FileAccess.open(save_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return false
	var text := file.get_as_text()
	file.close()

	var test_json := JSON.new()
	var err := test_json.parse(text)
	if err != OK or not (test_json.data is Dictionary):
		push_warning("File save hỏng hoặc sai cú pháp JSON, bỏ qua để bảo vệ game.")
		return false

	var data: Dictionary = test_json.data
	var version: String = str(data.get("save_version", ""))
	if not version in SUPPORTED_VERSIONS:
		push_warning("Phiên bản save_version không được hỗ trợ: " + version)
		return false

	# Kiểm tra các khối dữ liệu cốt lõi
	if not data.has("player") or not (data["player"] is Dictionary):
		push_warning("Thiếu dữ liệu player hợp lệ trong bản lưu.")
		return false
	if not data.has("quest") or not (data["quest"] is Dictionary):
		push_warning("Thiếu dữ liệu quest hợp lệ trong bản lưu.")
		return false

	# BƯỚC 1 (QUAN TRỌNG): Khôi phục Trang bị & Túi đồ TRƯỚC để tính toán lại Max Stats (Max MP / Sát thương)
	var p_eq := player.get_node_or_null("EquipmentComponent") as EquipmentComponent
	if p_eq:
		if data.has("equipment") and (data["equipment"] is Dictionary):
			p_eq.load_from_dict(data["equipment"])
		else:
			p_eq.load_from_dict({})

	var p_inv := player.get_node_or_null("InventoryComponent") as InventoryComponent
	if p_inv:
		if data.has("inventory") and (data["inventory"] is Array):
			p_inv.load_from_dict(data["inventory"])
		else:
			p_inv._initialize_slots()

	# BƯỚC 2: Khôi phục Player (Tọa độ & HP/MP)
	# Nhờ Bước 1 đã chạy, p_health.max_mana đã được cập nhật chính xác (80 nếu đeo moon_amulet, 60 nếu không)
	var p_data: Dictionary = data["player"]
	var raw_x = p_data.get("position_x", player.global_position.x)
	var raw_y = p_data.get("position_y", player.global_position.y)

	var px: float = float(raw_x) if typeof(raw_x) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else player.global_position.x
	var py: float = float(raw_y) if typeof(raw_y) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else player.global_position.y
	if not is_finite(px): px = 180.0
	if not is_finite(py): py = 630.0

	# Giới hạn tọa độ trong thế giới an toàn (clamp mở rộng cho Đấu trường Boss M4)
	px = clamp(px, 50.0, 6200.0)
	py = clamp(py, 100.0, 700.0)
	player.global_position = Vector2(px, py)

	var p_health := player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent
	if p_health:
		var raw_hp = p_data.get("current_health", p_health.max_health)
		var hp_val: float = float(raw_hp) if typeof(raw_hp) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else p_health.max_health
		p_health.current_health = clamp(hp_val, 0.0, p_health.max_health)

		var raw_mp = p_data.get("current_mana", p_health.max_mana)
		var mp_val: float = float(raw_mp) if typeof(raw_mp) in [TYPE_FLOAT, TYPE_INT] else p_health.max_mana
		p_health.current_mana = clamp(mp_val, 0.0, p_health.max_mana)

		p_health.emit_signal("health_changed", p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
		p_health.emit_signal("mana_changed", p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

	# BƯỚC 3: Khôi phục & Validate Vàng & Nhiệm vụ
	var raw_gold = data.get("gold", 0)
	var val_gold: int = int(raw_gold) if typeof(raw_gold) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0
	quest_mgr.gold = clamp(val_gold, 0, 999999)
	quest_mgr.emit_signal("gold_changed", quest_mgr.gold)

	var q_data: Dictionary = data["quest"]
	var raw_state = q_data.get("state", 0)
	var q_state: int = int(raw_state) if typeof(raw_state) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0
	q_state = clamp(q_state, 0, 3)

	var raw_amt = q_data.get("current_amount", 0)
	var q_amt: int = int(raw_amt) if typeof(raw_amt) in [TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT] else 0

	if quest_mgr.current_quest:
		quest_mgr.current_quest.current_state = q_state as QuestData.State
		quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount = clamp(q_amt, 0, quest_mgr.current_quest.target_amount)
		if q_state == QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
			quest_mgr.current_quest.current_amount = quest_mgr.current_quest.target_amount

		if ui:
			ui.update_quest_info(quest_mgr.current_quest)
			ui.update_gold(quest_mgr.gold)

	# BƯỚC 4: Khôi phục trạng thái vật phẩm thế giới và quái vật
	var raw_collected = data.get("collected_pickup_ids", [])
	var collected_ids: Array = raw_collected if raw_collected is Array else []

	var raw_dropped = data.get("dropped_pickups", [])
	var dropped_pickups: Array = raw_dropped if raw_dropped is Array else []

	var raw_defeated = data.get("defeated_enemy_ids", [])
	var defeated_ids: Array = raw_defeated if raw_defeated is Array else []

	if world.has_method("restore_world_state"):
		world.restore_world_state(collected_ids, dropped_pickups, defeated_ids)

	# BƯỚC 5: Khôi phục trạng thái Boss Lunareth (M4)
	var raw_boss = data.get("boss", {})
	var boss_dict: Dictionary = raw_boss if raw_boss is Dictionary else {}
	var b_activated: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("activated", false))
	var b_defeated: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("defeated", false))
	var b_reward: bool = bool(boss_dict.get("reward_claimed", false))

	if world.has_method("restore_boss_state"):
		world.restore_boss_state(b_activated, b_defeated, b_reward)

	# Đồng bộ danh sách quái đã chết vào QuestManager để chống đếm trùng
	for did in defeated_ids:
		var sid := str(did)
		if not sid in quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids:
			quest_mgr._killed_enemy_ids.append(sid)

	if ui:
		ui.show_notification("Đã tải bản lưu thành công!")

	return true


===================================================
FILE: src\ui\game_ui.gd
