class_name GameUI
extends CanvasLayer

## Giao diện người dùng chính (HUD, Dialogue, GameOver, PauseMenu, BossHUD, Victory) cho NOCTELYRA (M4)

signal retry_requested()
signal victory_dismissed()

@onready var hud: Control = $HUD
@onready var hp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/TopLeftBars/HPBar
@onready var mp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/TopLeftBars/MPBar
@onready var hp_label: Label = $HUD/TopLeftBars/HPBar/HPLabel
@onready var mp_label: Label = $HUD/TopLeftBars/MPBar/MPLabel
@onready var state_label: Label = $HUD/StateLabel
@onready var gold_label: Label = $HUD/GoldPanel/GoldLabel
@onready var quest_title_label: Label = $HUD/QuestPanel/VBox/QuestTitle
@onready var quest_progress_label: Label = $HUD/QuestPanel/VBox/QuestProgress
@onready var notification_label: Label = $HUD/NotificationLabel

# Boss UI (M4)
@onready var boss_hud: Control = $HUD/BossBarPanel
@onready var boss_name_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossNameLabel
@onready var boss_phase_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossPhaseLabel
@onready var boss_hp_bar: ProgressBar = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossHPBar
@onready var boss_hp_label: Label = $HUD/BossBarPanel/BossHPBar/BossHPLabel
@onready var boss_warning_label: Label = $HUD/BossWarningBanner
@onready var boss_announcement_label: Label = $HUD/BossAnnouncementBanner
@onready var victory_panel: Control = $VictoryPanel
@onready var victory_title: Label = $VictoryPanel/Panel/Title
@onready var victory_subtitle: Label = $VictoryPanel/Panel/Subtitle
@onready var victory_continue_btn: Button = $VictoryPanel/Panel/ContinueBtn

@onready var dialogue_box: Control = $DialogueBox
@onready var dialogue_speaker: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/SpeakerLabel
@onready var dialogue_text: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/DialogueText
@onready var dialogue_hint: Label = $DialogueBox/Panel/NextHint

@onready var game_over_panel: Control = $GameOverPanel
@onready var retry_button: Button = $GameOverPanel/Panel/RetryBtn

@onready var pause_menu: Control = $PauseMenu
@onready var save_btn: Button = $PauseMenu/Panel/SaveBtn
@onready var inventory_ui: InventoryUI = $InventoryUI

var _dialogue_lines: Array[String] = []
var _current_dialogue_idx: int = 0
var _current_quest_action: String = "none"
var _notif_timer: float = 0.0
var _dialogue_cooldown: float = 0.0
var _warning_timer: float = 0.0
var _announcement_timer: float = 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	if dialogue_box:
		dialogue_box.visible = false
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = false
	if notification_label:
		notification_label.visible = false
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = false
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.visible = false
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.visible = false
	if victory_panel:
		victory_panel.visible = false
	if retry_button:
		retry_button.pressed.connect(_on_retry_pressed)
	if save_btn:
		save_btn.pressed.connect(_on_save_pressed)
	if victory_continue_btn:
		victory_continue_btn.pressed.connect(_on_victory_continue_pressed)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _notif_timer > 0.0:
		_notif_timer -= delta
		if _notif_timer <= 0.0 and notification_label:
			notification_label.visible = false

	if _dialogue_cooldown > 0.0:
		_dialogue_cooldown -= delta

	if _warning_timer > 0.0:
		_warning_timer -= delta
		if _warning_timer <= 0.0 and boss_warning_label:
			boss_warning_label.visible = false

	if _announcement_timer > 0.0:
		_announcement_timer -= delta
		if _announcement_timer <= 0.0 and boss_announcement_label:
			boss_announcement_label.visible = false

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	# Mở/đóng Túi đồ bằng phím I
	if event.is_action_pressed("inventory"):
		if not is_dialogue_open():
			toggle_inventory()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Chặn các phím hành động khi đang mở hội thoại, trong thời gian hồi, hoặc đang mở túi đồ
	if is_dialogue_open() or is_dialogue_cooling_down() or is_inventory_open():
		if event.is_action_pressed("attack") or event.is_action_pressed("jump") or event.is_action_pressed("cast_spell") or event.is_action_pressed("dash"):
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
			return

	# Dự phòng nếu không có InteractionCoordinator trong scene
	if is_dialogue_open() and event.is_action_pressed("interact"):
		if get_tree() and get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("interaction_coordinator").size() == 0:
			advance_dialogue()
			get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func show_dialogue(speaker: String, lines: Array[String], action: String = "none") -> void:
	_dialogue_lines = lines
	_current_dialogue_idx = 0
	_current_quest_action = action
	dialogue_speaker.text = speaker
	_display_current_line()
	dialogue_box.visible = true

func _display_current_line() -> void:
	if _current_dialogue_idx < _dialogue_lines.size():
		dialogue_text.text = _dialogue_lines[_current_dialogue_idx]
		if _current_dialogue_idx == _dialogue_lines.size() - 1:
			dialogue_hint.text = "[E để đóng]"
		else:
			dialogue_hint.text = "[E để tiếp tục]"

func advance_dialogue() -> void:
	_current_dialogue_idx += 1
	if _current_dialogue_idx < _dialogue_lines.size():
		_display_current_line()
	else:
		close_dialogue()

func close_dialogue() -> void:
	dialogue_box.visible = false
	_dialogue_cooldown = 0.25 # Grace cooldown 0.25s
	var scene: Node = _get_active_scene()
	var quest_mgr: QuestManager = scene.get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager if scene else null
	var player: Player = scene.get_node_or_null("Player") as Player if scene else null
	var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent") as HealthComponent if player else null

	if quest_mgr:
		if _current_quest_action == "start_quest":
			quest_mgr.start_quest()
		elif _current_quest_action == "complete_quest":
			quest_mgr.complete_quest(p_health)
			# Tự động lưu sau khi hoàn thành nhiệm vụ và nhận thưởng
			SaveManager.save_game(scene)
	_current_quest_action = "none"

func _get_active_scene() -> Node:
	if get_tree() and get_tree().current_scene:
		return get_tree().current_scene
	return get_parent()

func is_dialogue_open() -> bool:
	return dialogue_box and dialogue_box.visible

func is_dialogue_cooling_down() -> bool:
	return _dialogue_cooldown > 0.0

func toggle_inventory() -> void:
	if inventory_ui:
		inventory_ui.toggle()

func open_inventory() -> void:
	if inventory_ui:
		inventory_ui.open()

func close_inventory() -> void:
	if inventory_ui:
		inventory_ui.close()

func is_inventory_open() -> bool:
	return inventory_ui != null and inventory_ui.is_open()

func _on_save_pressed() -> void:
	var scene := _get_active_scene()
	# Chặn lưu game thủ công khi Boss đang giao chiến (Yêu cầu F)
	if scene and scene.has_method("is_boss_battle_active") and scene.is_boss_battle_active():
		show_notification("Không thể lưu game trong khi đang giao tranh với Boss!")
		return
	SaveManager.save_game(scene)

func update_player_hp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if hp_bar:
		hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		hp_bar.value = cur
	if hp_label:
		hp_label.text = "HP: %d/%d" % [int(cur), int(max_v)]

func update_player_mp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if mp_bar:
		mp_bar.max_value = max_v
		mp_bar.value = cur
	if mp_label:
		mp_label.text = "MP: %d/%d" % [int(cur), int(max_v)]

func update_player_state(st_name: String) -> void:
	if state_label:
		state_label.text = "State: %s" % st_name

func update_gold(amount: int) -> void:
	if gold_label:
		gold_label.text = "Vàng: %d G" % amount

func update_quest_info(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	if quest == null:
		return
	if quest_title_label:
		quest_title_label.text = "Nhiệm vụ: %s" % quest.title
	if quest_progress_label:
		match quest.current_state:
			QuestData.State.NOT_STARTED:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiến độ: Chưa nhận (Gặp Elyra)"
			QuestData.State.ACTIVE:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiêu diệt sinh vật: %d/%d" % [quest.current_amount, quest.target_amount]
			QuestData.State.READY_TO_TURN_IN:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Tiến độ: 3/3 (Về gặp Elyra nhận thưởng!)"
			QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
				quest_progress_label.text = "Trạng thái: Hoàn thành! (Cổng Đấu trường đã mở)"

func show_notification(msg: String) -> void:
	if notification_label:
		notification_label.text = msg
		notification_label.visible = true
		_notif_timer = 3.5

func show_game_over() -> void:
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = true

func hide_game_over() -> void:
	if game_over_panel:
		game_over_panel.visible = false

func _on_retry_pressed() -> void:
	hide_game_over()
	emit_signal("retry_requested")

# --- HỆ THỐNG GIAO DIỆN BOSS (M4) ---

func show_boss_hud() -> void:
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = true

func hide_boss_hud() -> void:
	if boss_hud:
		boss_hud.visible = false
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.visible = false
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.visible = false

func update_boss_hp(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if boss_hp_bar:
		boss_hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		boss_hp_bar.value = cur
	if boss_hp_label:
		boss_hp_label.text = "HP: %d/%d" % [int(max(0, cur)), int(max_v)]

func update_boss_phase(phase: int) -> void:
	if boss_phase_label:
		if phase == 2:
			boss_phase_label.text = "[GIAI ĐOẠN 2 - HẮC NGUYỆT]"
			boss_phase_label.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.4, 0.9, 1.0)
			show_boss_announcement("LUNARETH GIẢI PHÓNG HẮC NGUYỆT MA LỰC!")
		else:
			boss_phase_label.text = "[GIAI ĐOẠN 1]"
			boss_phase_label.modulate = Color(0.4, 0.9, 1.0, 1.0)

func show_boss_warning(skill_name: String, duration: float) -> void:
	if boss_warning_label:
		boss_warning_label.text = "⚠️ CẢNH BÁO: %s!" % skill_name.to_upper()
		boss_warning_label.visible = true
		_warning_timer = max(0.6, duration)

func show_boss_announcement(msg: String, duration: float = 2.5) -> void:
	if boss_announcement_label:
		boss_announcement_label.text = msg
		boss_announcement_label.visible = true
		_announcement_timer = duration

func show_victory_banner(title: String, subtitle: String) -> void:
	hide_boss_hud()
	if victory_panel:
		if victory_title: victory_title.text = title
		if victory_subtitle: victory_subtitle.text = subtitle
		victory_panel.visible = true

func hide_victory_banner() -> void:
	if victory_panel:
		victory_panel.visible = false

func _on_victory_continue_pressed() -> void:
	hide_victory_banner()
	emit_signal("victory_dismissed")


===================================================

FILE: src\ui\main_menu.gd

class_name MainMenu
extends Control

## Màn hình chính (Main Menu) cho NOCTELYRA (M3)
## Cung cấp Continue (nếu có save), New Game (kèm xác nhận ghi đè), và Quit.

const GAME_SCENE_PATH: String = "res://src/world/noctis_forest.tscn"

@onready var continue_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/ContinueBtn
@onready var new_game_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/NewGameBtn
@onready var quit_btn: Button = $Panel/VBox/QuitBtn

@onready var confirm_dialog: Control = $ConfirmDialog
@onready var confirm_yes_btn: Button = $ConfirmDialog/Panel/HBox/YesBtn
@onready var confirm_no_btn: Button = $ConfirmDialog/Panel/HBox/NoBtn

func _ready() -> void:
	if confirm_dialog:
		confirm_dialog.visible = false

	_update_buttons()

	if continue_btn:
		continue_btn.pressed.connect(_on_continue_pressed)
	if new_game_btn:
		new_game_btn.pressed.connect(_on_new_game_pressed)
	if quit_btn:
		quit_btn.pressed.connect(_on_quit_pressed)

	if confirm_yes_btn:
		confirm_yes_btn.pressed.connect(_on_confirm_new_game)
	if confirm_no_btn:
		confirm_no_btn.pressed.connect(func(): confirm_dialog.visible = false)

func _update_buttons() -> void:
	var has_valid_save := SaveManager.has_save()
	if continue_btn:
		continue_btn.disabled = not has_valid_save

func _on_continue_pressed() -> void:
	if SaveManager.has_save():
		SaveManager.should_load_on_start = true
		get_tree().change_scene_to_file(GAME_SCENE_PATH)

func _on_new_game_pressed() -> void:
	if SaveManager.has_save():
		if confirm_dialog:
			confirm_dialog.visible = true
	else:
		_start_new_game()

func _on_confirm_new_game() -> void:
	SaveManager.delete_save()
	_start_new_game()

func _start_new_game() -> void:
	SaveManager.should_load_on_start = false
	get_tree().change_scene_to_file(GAME_SCENE_PATH)

func _on_quit_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().quit()

FILE: src\ui\pause_controller.gd
===================================================

class_name PauseController
extends Node

@export var pause_menu: Control

func _ready() -> void:
	# PauseController luôn luôn xử lý input bất kể game đang pause hay không
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = false

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed("pause"):
		# Nếu túi đồ đang mở thì Esc đóng túi đồ trước thay vì mở PauseMenu
		var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur_scene:
			var ui := cur_scene.get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
			if ui and ui.is_inventory_open():
				ui.close_inventory()
				get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
				return

		toggle_pause()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()

func toggle_pause() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	if tree.paused:
		resume()
	else:
		pause()

func pause() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	tree.paused = true
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = true

func resume() -> void:
	var tree := get_tree()
	if tree == null:
		return
	tree.paused = false
	if pause_menu:
		pause_menu.visible = false


===================================================
FILE: src\world\magic_gate.gd
===================================================

class_name MagicGate
extends StaticBody2D

## Cổng Ma Pháp chặn lối vào Đấu trường Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Chỉ mở khóa sau khi hoàn thành và trả nhiệm vụ tiêu diệt quái vật cho Elyra.
## Ngăn cản tuyệt đối việc vượt qua bằng Dash hay Double Jump khi đang khóa.

signal gate_opened()

@export var is_open: bool = false

@onready var collision_shape: CollisionShape2D = $CollisionShape2D
@onready var visual_barrier: ColorRect = $VisualBarrier
@onready var runes: Label = $VisualBarrier/RuneLabel
@onready var interaction_area: Area2D = $InteractionArea

func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	if is_open:
		open_gate(false)
	else:
		close_gate()
	if interaction_area:
		interaction_area.body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)

func open_gate(notify: bool = true) -> void:
	is_open = true
	if collision_shape:
		collision_shape.disabled = true
	if visual_barrier:
		visual_barrier.color = Color(0.2, 0.8, 1.0, 0.2)
	if runes:
		runes.text = "[PHONG ẤN ĐÃ GIẢI TRỪ]"
		runes.modulate = Color(0.4, 1.0, 0.8, 0.8)
	emit_signal("gate_opened")

func close_gate() -> void:
	is_open = false
	if collision_shape:
		collision_shape.disabled = false
	if visual_barrier:
		visual_barrier.color = Color(0.65, 0.15, 0.85, 0.85)
	if runes:
		runes.text = "[PHONG ẤN CỔ TỰ]\nCần hoàn thành nhiệm vụ Elyra!"
		runes.modulate = Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.9, 1.0)

func update_quest_status(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	if quest and quest.current_state == QuestData.State.COMPLETED:
		if not is_open:
			open_gate(true)
	else:
		if is_open:
			close_gate()

func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player and not is_open:
		var cur_scene = get_tree().current_scene if get_tree() else null
		if cur_scene and "game_ui" in cur_scene and cur_scene.game_ui:
			cur_scene.game_ui.show_notification("Cổng Ma Pháp bị phong ấn! Hãy hoàn thành nhiệm vụ của Elyra để giải trừ.")

===================================================
FILE: src\world\noctis_forest.gd

class_name NoctisForest
extends Node2D

## Thế giới Bìa Rừng Noctis & Đấu trường Boss Lunareth (M4)
## Quản lý vòng lặp nhiệm vụ, quái vật, cổng ma pháp, đấu trường và Boss Lunareth.

var player: Player
var game_ui: GameUI
var quest_manager: QuestManager
var pause_controller: PauseController
var npc_elyra: NPC
var interaction_coordinator: InteractionCoordinator

# Boss & Đấu trường (M4)
const BossLunarethScript = preload("res://src/boss/boss_lunareth.gd")
const MagicGateScript = preload("res://src/world/magic_gate.gd")

var boss_lunareth: CharacterBody2D
var magic_gate: StaticBody2D
var arena_barrier: StaticBody2D
var arena_trigger: Area2D
var arena_checkpoint: Vector2 = Vector2(4120.0, 630.0)

var defeated_enemy_ids: Array = []
var collected_pickup_ids: Array = []

var boss_battle_started: bool = false
var boss_defeated: bool = false
var boss_reward_claimed: bool = false

func _enter_tree() -> void:
	init_references()

func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	init_references()
	if SaveManager.should_load_on_start:
		SaveManager.should_load_on_start = false
		SaveManager.load_game(self)

func init_references() -> void:
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if game_ui == null:
		game_ui = get_node_or_null("GameUI") as GameUI
	if quest_manager == null:
		quest_manager = get_node_or_null("QuestManager") as QuestManager
	if pause_controller == null:
		pause_controller = get_node_or_null("PauseController") as PauseController
	if npc_elyra == null:
		npc_elyra = get_node_or_null("NPC_Elyra") as NPC
	if interaction_coordinator == null:
		interaction_coordinator = get_node_or_null("InteractionCoordinator") as InteractionCoordinator

	# Các thực thể Boss M4
	if magic_gate == null:
		magic_gate = get_node_or_null("MagicGate") as StaticBody2D
	if boss_lunareth == null:
		boss_lunareth = get_node_or_null("BossLunareth") as CharacterBody2D
	if arena_barrier == null:
		arena_barrier = get_node_or_null("Geometry/ArenaBarrier") as StaticBody2D
	if arena_trigger == null:
		arena_trigger = get_node_or_null("ArenaTrigger") as Area2D

	# Khởi tạo trạng thái rào chắn đấu trường
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = false
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = true

	# Kết nối Player
	if player and not player.state_changed.is_connected(_on_player_state_changed):
		player.add_to_group("players")
		player.state_changed.connect(_on_player_state_changed)
		_on_player_state_changed("IDLE")

		var p_health: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
		if p_health and not p_health.health_changed.is_connected(_on_player_health_changed):
			p_health.health_changed.connect(_on_player_health_changed)
			p_health.mana_changed.connect(_on_player_mana_changed)
			if game_ui:
				game_ui.update_player_hp(p_health.current_health, p_health.max_health)
				game_ui.update_player_mp(p_health.current_mana, p_health.max_mana)

	# Kết nối GameUI
	if game_ui and not game_ui.retry_requested.is_connected(_on_retry_requested):
		game_ui.retry_requested.connect(_on_retry_requested)
		if player and game_ui.inventory_ui:
			game_ui.inventory_ui.setup_player(player)
		if quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
			game_ui.update_quest_info(quest_manager.current_quest)
			game_ui.update_gold(quest_manager.gold)

	# Kết nối QuestManager
	if quest_manager and not quest_manager.quest_started.is_connected(_on_quest_updated):
		quest_manager.quest_started.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		quest_manager.quest_progress_updated.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		quest_manager.quest_ready_to_turn_in.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		quest_manager.quest_completed.connect(_on_quest_updated)
		quest_manager.gold_changed.connect(_on_gold_changed)
		quest_manager.notification_posted.connect(_on_notification_posted)

	# Kết nối NPC Elyra
	if npc_elyra and not npc_elyra.dialogue_requested.is_connected(_on_npc_dialogue_requested):
		npc_elyra.dialogue_requested.connect(_on_npc_dialogue_requested)

	# Kết nối các quái vật trên map
	var enemies = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies")
	for e in enemies:
		if e is Enemy:
			if quest_manager and not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(quest_manager.register_enemy_kill):
				e.enemy_defeated.connect(quest_manager.register_enemy_kill)
			if not e.enemy_defeated.is_connected(_on_enemy_defeated):
				e.enemy_defeated.connect(_on_enemy_defeated)

	# Kết nối Boss Lunareth (M4)
	if boss_lunareth and not boss_lunareth.boss_health_changed.is_connected(_on_boss_health_changed):
		boss_lunareth.boss_health_changed.connect(_on_boss_health_changed)
		boss_lunareth.boss_phase_changed.connect(_on_boss_phase_changed)
		boss_lunareth.boss_telegraph_started.connect(_on_boss_telegraph_started)
		boss_lunareth.boss_defeated.connect(_on_boss_defeated)

	# Kết nối Arena Trigger
	if arena_trigger and not arena_trigger.body_entered.is_connected(_on_arena_trigger_entered):
		arena_trigger.body_entered.connect(_on_arena_trigger_entered)

	# Đồng bộ trạng thái mở cổng theo nhiệm vụ
	if magic_gate and quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
		magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest_manager.current_quest)

func is_boss_battle_active() -> bool:
	return boss_battle_started and not boss_defeated and boss_lunareth != null and boss_lunareth.current_state != BossLunarethScript.State.DEFEATED

func _on_arena_trigger_entered(body: Node) -> void:
	if body is Player and not boss_defeated and not boss_battle_started:
		start_boss_battle()

func start_boss_battle() -> void:
	boss_battle_started = true

	# Kích hoạt rào chắn đấu trường (Arena Boundary) khóa chặt lối vào
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = true
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = false

	# Cập nhật checkpoint hồi sinh ngoài cổng đấu trường
	if player:
		player.spawn_position = arena_checkpoint

	# Đánh thức Boss
	if boss_lunareth:
		boss_lunareth.activate_boss()

	# Hiển thị HUD Boss
	if game_ui and boss_lunareth:
		game_ui.show_boss_hud()
		if boss_lunareth.health_component:
			game_ui.update_boss_hp(boss_lunareth.health_component.current_health, boss_lunareth.health_component.max_health)
		game_ui.update_boss_phase(1)
		game_ui.show_notification("Linh Lộc Lunareth xuất hiện!")

func _on_boss_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_boss_hp(cur, max_v)

func _on_boss_phase_changed(new_phase: int) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_boss_phase(new_phase)

func _on_boss_telegraph_started(skill_name: String, duration: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_boss_warning(skill_name, duration)

func _on_boss_defeated() -> void:
	boss_battle_started = false
	boss_defeated = true

	# Mở khóa rào chắn đấu trường
	if arena_barrier:
		arena_barrier.visible = false
		var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
		if b_col:
			b_col.disabled = true

	grant_boss_reward()

func grant_boss_reward() -> void:
	if boss_reward_claimed:
		return
	boss_reward_claimed = true

	# 1. Thưởng 100 Vàng
	if quest_manager:
		quest_manager.gold += 100
		quest_manager.emit_signal("gold_changed", quest_manager.gold)

	# 2. Thưởng vật phẩm Mảnh Nguyệt Thực (eclipse_fragment)
	if player and player.inventory_component:
		var inv: InventoryComponent = player.inventory_component
		if inv.can_add_item("eclipse_fragment", 1):
			inv.add_item("eclipse_fragment", 1)
			if game_ui:
				game_ui.show_notification("Đã nhận Mảnh Nguyệt Thực & 100 Vàng!")
		else:
			# Túi đầy: rơi đồ dưới dạng ItemPickup trên mặt đất tại vị trí Boss
			_spawn_boss_reward_pickup()
			if game_ui:
				game_ui.show_notification("Túi đồ đầy! Mảnh Nguyệt Thực đã rơi trên mặt đất.")

	# 3. Hiển thị thông báo chiến thắng & thanh tẩy thành công
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_victory_banner(
			"THANH TẨY THÀNH CÔNG!",
			"Linh Lộc Lunareth đã hồi phục tâm trí!\nRừng Noctis đã được thanh tẩy.\nNhận thưởng: 100 Vàng & Mảnh Nguyệt Thực."
		)

	# 4. Tự động lưu tiến trình game sau chiến thắng
	SaveManager.save_game(self)

func _spawn_boss_reward_pickup() -> void:
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	var p := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
	p.pickup_id = "drop_boss_eclipse_fragment"
	p.item_id = "eclipse_fragment"
	p.amount = 1
	p.is_dynamic = true
	var drop_x: float = boss_lunareth.global_position.x if boss_lunareth else 5300.0
	p.global_position = Vector2(drop_x, 630.0)
	add_child(p)

func _on_enemy_defeated(enemy_node: Node) -> void:
	if enemy_node is Enemy and not enemy_node.enemy_id.is_empty():
		if not enemy_node.enemy_id in defeated_enemy_ids:
			defeated_enemy_ids.append(enemy_node.enemy_id)

func register_collected_pickup(p_id: String) -> void:
	if not p_id.is_empty() and not p_id in collected_pickup_ids:
		collected_pickup_ids.append(p_id)

func get_dropped_pickups_data() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var result: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var dyn_pickups = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("dynamic_pickups")
	for p in dyn_pickups:
		if p is ItemPickup and is_instance_valid(p) and not p.is_picked_up and not p.is_queued_for_deletion():
			result.append({
				"pickup_id": p.pickup_id,
				"item_id": p.item_id,
				"amount": p.amount,
				"x": p.global_position.x,
				"y": p.global_position.y
			})
	return result

func restore_world_state(p_collected_ids: Array, p_dropped_pickups: Array, p_defeated_ids: Array) -> void:
	collected_pickup_ids.clear()
	for id in p_collected_ids:
		collected_pickup_ids.append(str(id))

	defeated_enemy_ids.clear()
	for id in p_defeated_ids:
		defeated_enemy_ids.append(str(id))

	# 1. Dọn các vật phẩm đặt sẵn đã từng nhặt
	var preplaced = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("preplaced_pickups")
	for p in preplaced:
		if p is ItemPickup and not p.pickup_id.is_empty():
			if p.pickup_id in collected_pickup_ids:
				p.queue_free()

	# 2. Xóa các dynamic pickup cũ trước khi phục hồi danh sách lưu (tránh nhân bản)
	var existing_dyn = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("dynamic_pickups")
	for p in existing_dyn:
		if is_instance_valid(p):
			p.queue_free()

	# 3. Phục hồi các vật phẩm quái rơi chưa nhặt
	var pickup_scene: PackedScene = preload("res://src/loot/item_pickup.tscn")
	for dp in p_dropped_pickups:
		if dp is Dictionary:
			var p := pickup_scene.instantiate() as ItemPickup
			p.pickup_id = str(dp.get("pickup_id", ""))
			p.item_id = str(dp.get("item_id", "mana_shard"))
			p.amount = max(1, int(dp.get("amount", 1)))
			p.is_dynamic = true
			var px: float = float(dp.get("x", 0.0))
			var py: float = float(dp.get("y", 0.0))
			p.global_position = Vector2(px, py)
			add_child(p)

	# 4. Xóa các quái đã chết
	var enemies = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("enemies")
	for e in enemies:
		if e is Enemy and not e.enemy_id.is_empty():
			if e.enemy_id in defeated_enemy_ids:
				e.queue_free()

func restore_boss_state(p_activated: bool, p_defeated: bool, p_reward: bool) -> void:
	boss_defeated = p_defeated
	boss_reward_claimed = p_reward

	if boss_defeated:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if magic_gate:
			magic_gate.open_gate(false)
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.purify_boss()
		if game_ui:
			game_ui.hide_boss_hud()
	else:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.reset_boss()
		if magic_gate and quest_manager and quest_manager.current_quest:
			magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest_manager.current_quest)

func _on_player_state_changed(st_name: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_state(st_name)

func _on_player_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_hp(cur, max_v)

func _on_player_mana_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_player_mp(cur, max_v)

func _on_quest_updated(quest: QuestData) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_quest_info(quest)
	# Mở cổng đấu trường ngay khi nhiệm vụ hoàn thành
	if magic_gate:
		magic_gate.update_quest_status(quest)

func _on_gold_changed(new_gold: int) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.update_gold(new_gold)

func _on_notification_posted(msg: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_notification(msg)

func _on_npc_dialogue_requested(npc: NPC, lines: Array[String], action: String) -> void:
	if game_ui:
		game_ui.show_dialogue(npc.npc_name, lines, action)

func _on_retry_requested() -> void:
	if player == null:
		player = get_node_or_null("Player") as Player
	if player:
		player.respawn()

	# Xử lý Retry khi tử trận trong trận Boss (Yêu cầu F)
	if boss_battle_started and not boss_defeated:
		boss_battle_started = false
		if arena_barrier:
			arena_barrier.visible = false
			var b_col := arena_barrier.get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D") as CollisionShape2D
			if b_col:
				b_col.disabled = true
		if boss_lunareth:
			boss_lunareth.reset_boss()
		if game_ui:
			game_ui.hide_boss_hud()

func _on_resume_button_pressed() -> void:
	if pause_controller:
		pause_controller.resume()

func _on_quit_button_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().quit()


===================================================
FILE: src\world\noctis_forest.tscn

[gd_scene load_steps=16 format=3 uid="uid://c7v1n8m3q2k4"]

===================================================
FILE: src\world\test_level.gd
===================================================

extends Node2D

@onready var player: Player = $Player
@onready var state_label: Label = $CanvasLayer/HUD/StateLabel
@onready var hp_bar: ProgressBar = $CanvasLayer/HUD/VBoxBars/HPBar
@onready var mp_bar: ProgressBar = $CanvasLayer/HUD/VBoxBars/MPBar
@onready var pause_controller: PauseController = $PauseController

func _ready() -> void:
	# Gameplay level đặt PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE

	if player:
		# Thêm Player vào group "players" để Enemy dễ nhận diện
		player.add_to_group("players")
		player.state_changed.connect(_on_player_state_changed)
		_on_player_state_changed("IDLE")

		var health_comp: HealthComponent = player.get_node_or_null("HealthComponent")
		if health_comp:
			health_comp.health_changed.connect(_on_player_health_changed)
			health_comp.mana_changed.connect(_on_player_mana_changed)
			_on_player_health_changed(health_comp.current_health, health_comp.max_health)
			_on_player_mana_changed(health_comp.current_mana, health_comp.max_mana)

func _on_player_state_changed(new_state: String) -> void:
	if state_label:
		state_label.text = "State: %s" % new_state

func _on_player_health_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if hp_bar:
		hp_bar.max_value = max_v
		hp_bar.value = cur

func _on_player_mana_changed(cur: float, max_v: float) -> void:
	if mp_bar:
		mp_bar.max_value = max_v
		mp_bar.value = cur

func _on_resume_button_pressed() -> void:
	var ctrl = pause_controller if pause_controller else get_node_or_null("PauseController")
	if ctrl:
		ctrl.resume()
	else:
		get_tree().paused = false
		var menu = get_node_or_null("CanvasLayer/PauseMenu")
		if menu:
			menu.visible = false

func _on_quit_button_pressed() -> void:
	get_tree().quit()
